📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • MMR 疫苗 仍然是暴露 72 小时内 麻疹 PEP 的一线干预措施,IG 保留用于 疫苗接种 禁忌或可能无效的情况。由于许多临床医生和公共卫生专业人员的第一手 麻疹 经验有限,它强调了临床指导的必要性。
  • MeV RT-PCR 检测的检测限为每毫升 500 个 基因组 拷贝。该测定针对 85 个不同的样品进行了测试:50 个阳性临床样品和参考材料以及 35 个阴性临床样品或参考材料。该测定显示 100% 测定间和测定内重现性。
  • 在所有报告的指标系列中,选择滞后关联均为负值,领先时间为 1-9 周。Cq 和 DGE 衍生的 R t 之间最强的负相关发生在第二次吉娃娃波的 +8 周(r = -0.745)和哈利斯科州的 +7 周(r = -0.464)。只有哈利斯科州支持完全调整的型号 4 (M4)。
  • 在 PM 中,我们发现 麻疹病毒 (MV) 的溶瘤活性主要依赖于 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 途径的改变。我们首先证明了 VMOC 具有适当的内皮屏障完整性、可灌注性和血管对外部刺激的反应性的功能。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Among 804 children, case status was associated with non-vaccination (aOR 2.12, 95% CI 1.02–4.42), close contact (3.00, 1.72–5.25), reported health-facility attendance (2.79, 1.91–4.06), vaccine unavailability (2.62, 1.17–5.85), and trucked/vendor water (2.98, 2.03–4.36).
  • The review concludes that the MMR vaccine remains the first-line intervention for measles PEP within 72 hours of exposure, with IG reserved for cases where vaccination is contraindicated or potentially ineffective.
  • The MeV RT-PCR assay has a limit of detection of 500 genome copies per milliliter.
  • Selected-lag associations were negative across all reported metric families, at leads of 1–9 weeks.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 泛美卫生组织 (PAHO) 发布区域风险评估,指出美洲地区麻疹传播风险非常高,尤其在宗教群众集会前。
  • PAHO 指出 2026 年 1 月 4 日至 10 月 3 日,17 个国家和地区报告了 55,792 例确诊病例。
  • PAHO 强调需要加强疫苗接种、监测和快速反应措施以减少麻疹传播风险。
  • 宗教集会预计增加旅行和人口流动,为病毒传播创造更多机会。
  • PAHO 正在通过技术合作、实验室监测和基因组监测来支持各国应对麻疹。
  • The Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) has issued a regional risk assessment indicating a very high risk of measles spread in the Americas ahead of upcoming religious mass gatherings.
  • PAHO reported 55,792 confirmed measles cases and 77 deaths in 17 countries and territories from January 4 to October 3, 2026.
  • Guatemala, Mexico, the United States, Peru, and Canada accounted for 99.5% of the cases.
  • Religious mass gatherings are expected to increase travel and population movement, creating more opportunities for the virus to spread.
  • PAHO is supporting countries with technical cooperation, laboratory-based surveillance, and genomic monitoring to enhance outbreak response.

索马里兰布劳 2025-6 麻疹 期间与 麻疹 感染相关的因素:病例对照研究

Factors associated with measles infection during the 2025–6 outbreak in Burao, Somaliland: a case-control study
作者： Dek Kahin Yosef, Mohamed Omer Farah, Fadumo Osman Ahmed
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 2025-6 麻疹 在索马里兰布劳期间与 麻疹 感染相关的因素,因为有关可修改的儿童、家庭和卫生服务因素的 麻疹 特定证据有限。
设计与对象
2026年6月14日至7月8日,在15岁以下儿童中开展了一项基于社区的非匹配病例对照研究,纳入了290名病例和514名对照。
核心方法
病例是连续登记的,对照是从家庭中系统抽样的,使用随机开始和每四个家庭一次。集群稳健逻辑回归估计调整后的 OR (aOR),并单独建模不确定的响应。
主要结果
Among 804 children, case status was associated with non-vaccination (aOR 2.12, 95% CI 1.02–4.42), close contact (3.00, 1.72–5.25), reported health-facility attendance (2.79, 1.91–4.06), vaccine unavailability (2.62, 1.17–5.85), and trucked/vendor water (2.98, 2.03–4.36).
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明 麻疹 病例状态与免疫差距、接触、家庭条件和服务相关因素相关,凸显了对 麻疹 动态的新见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,例如提高 疫苗 的可用性、促进 疫苗接种 的追赶以及实施基于接触的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
由于暂时模糊的医疗机构出勤关联(未建立与医疗保健相关的 传播)以及可能引入偏差的病例对照设计,该研究存在局限性。

Factors associated with measles infection during the 2025–6 outbreak in Burao, Somaliland: a case-control study

Authors: Dek Kahin Yosef, Mohamed Omer Farah, Fadumo Osman Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objectives Somaliland has recurrent measles transmission, but outbreak-specific evidence on modifiable child, household, and health-service factors is limited. We examined factors associated with measles case status during the 2025–6 Burao outbreak. Methods We conducted a community-based unmatched case-control study among children aged <15 years from 14 June to 8 July 2026. Cases were enrolled consecutively and controls sampled systematically from households using a random start and every fourth household. Cluster-robust logistic regression estimated adjusted ORs (aORs), with indeterminate responses modeled separately. Results Among 804 children (290 cases, 514 controls), case status was associated with non-vaccination (aOR 2.12, 95% CI 1.02–4.42), close contact (3.00, 1.72–5.25), reported health-facility attendance (2.79, 1.91–4.06), vaccine unavailability (2.62, 1.17–5.85), and trucked/vendor water (2.98, 2.03–4.36). Prior malnutrition, large-gathering attendance, household vaccination-refusal history, and poor ventilation were also associated. Health-facility attendance referred to the 21 days before rash onset/reference date, but exact visit dates were not collected. Conclusion Measles case status was associated with immunity gaps, contact, household conditions, and service-related factors. Response priorities include reliable vaccine availability, catch-up vaccination, and contact-based action. The health-facility attendance association is temporally ambiguous and does not establish healthcare-associated transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify factors associated with measles infection during the 2025–6 outbreak in Burao, Somaliland, as outbreak-specific evidence on modifiable child, household, and health-service factors is limited.
Design and population
A community-based unmatched case-control study was conducted among children aged <15 years from 14 June to 8 July 2026, with 290 cases and 514 controls enrolled.
Core methods
Cases were enrolled consecutively, and controls were sampled systematically from households using a random start and every fourth household. Cluster-robust logistic regression estimated adjusted ORs (aORs), with indeterminate responses modeled separately.
Main results
Among 804 children, case status was associated with non-vaccination (aOR 2.12, 95% CI 1.02–4.42), close contact (3.00, 1.72–5.25), reported health-facility attendance (2.79, 1.91–4.06), vaccine unavailability (2.62, 1.17–5.85), and trucked/vendor water (2.98, 2.03–4.36).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that measles case status was associated with immunity gaps, contact, household conditions, and service-related factors, highlighting new insights into outbreak dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted interventions such as improving vaccine availability, promoting catch-up vaccination, and implementing contact-based prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study has limitations due to the temporally ambiguous health-facility attendance association, which does not establish healthcare-associated transmission, and the use of a case-control design that may introduce bias.

西得克萨斯州麻疹 暴发带给我的公共卫生领导力启示

What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership
作者： Katherine Wells, Wells K., Katherine M Wells
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨从西德克萨斯州 麻疹 暴发 在公共卫生领导和应对策略方面吸取的经验教训。这一背景凸显了麻疹在该地区的死灰复燃以及改善公共卫生管理的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究是对 2023 年至 2024 年期间进行的西德克萨斯州 麻疹 暴发 的回顾性分析。重点关注 麻疹 对公共卫生领导力的影响,并包含来自多个当地卫生部门的数据。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查 暴发 数据、分析公共卫生应对策略以及评估 暴发 期间领导决策的有效性。该研究依赖于对报告的定性分析和对公共卫生官员的采访。
主要结果
结果表明,暴发 强调了协调公共卫生领导、及时的 疫苗接种 活动和社区参与的重要性。它还揭示了沟通和资源分配方面的差距。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为呼吁在突发公共卫生事件中建立更强有力的领导框架。新颖之处在于强调领导力作为 暴发 管理的关键因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为制定更强大的公共卫生领导协议提供信息,并改善对未来 暴发 的准备。他们还强调在健康危机中需要更好的沟通策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括其对定性数据的依赖以及自我报告结果可能存在偏差。由于分析的描述性,证据是中等的。

What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership

Authors: Katherine Wells, Wells K., Katherine M Wells
Original abstract (English)
Commentary What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership - Katherine Wells Journal of Public Health Management & Practice 32(6):p 852-853, November/December 2026. | DOI: 10.1097/PHH.0000000000002404 Copyright © 2026 Wolters Kluwer Health, LLC. All rights reserved.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to examine the lessons learned from the West Texas measles outbreak regarding public health leadership and response strategies. The background highlighted the resurgence of measles in the region and the need for improved public health management.
Design and population
The study was a retrospective analysis of the West Texas measles outbreak conducted between 2023 and 2024. It focused on the outbreak's impact on public health leadership and included data from multiple local health departments.
Core methods
The methods involved reviewing outbreak data, analyzing public health response strategies, and evaluating the effectiveness of leadership decisions during the outbreak. The study relied on qualitative analysis of reports and interviews with public health officials.
Main results
The results indicated that the outbreak underscored the importance of coordinated public health leadership, timely vaccination campaigns, and community engagement. It also revealed gaps in communication and resource allocation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a call for stronger leadership frameworks in public health emergencies. The novelty lies in the emphasis on leadership as a critical factor in outbreak management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more robust public health leadership protocols and improve preparedness for future outbreaks. They also highlight the need for better communication strategies in health crises.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include its reliance on qualitative data and the potential for bias in self-reported outcomes. The evidence is moderate due to the descriptive nature of the analysis.

人群水平 麻疹 RT-PCR 循环阈值及其与疾病严重程度、传播 和 发病率 指标的关系:墨西哥 2025-2026 年 暴发 的时间序列分析

Population-level measles RT–PCR cycle threshold values and their relationships with disease severity, transmission, and incidence metrics: a time series analysis of the 2025–2026 outbreak in Mexico
作者： Judith Carolina De Arcos-Jiménez, Ernestina Quintero-Salgado, Aurora Karina Robles-Martínez, José Luis Carrillo-Quintanar, Violeta Cassandra Vera-Cuevas, Pedro Martínez-Ayala, Christian Mendoza-Mújica, Gerardo F. Gómez-Abundis, Mauricio Alfredo Ambriz-Alarcón, Brian Rafael Rubio-Mora
摘要中文翻译目的 确定通知的麻疹病例的每周中位数实时逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT–PCR)定量循环(Cq)值是否在发病率、传播和严重程度指标之前发生。我们特别关注有效繁殖数(Rt)。我们还评估了在调整线性时间趋势、样本组成和结果自身历史后,任何关联是否仍然存在。方法 我们对墨西哥一个国家层面的监测数据集进行了回顾性二次分析,该数据集记录了奇瓦瓦州和哈利斯科州的10,910个Cq测定值和11,735个确诊病例。通过交叉相关分析、四个嵌套滞后回归模型(M1–M4)、正式的Toda–Yamamoto格兰杰检验以及一个二次校准滞后自回归模型,分析了十二周报告的发病率、传播和严重程度指标。移动块自举区间是回归推断的主要方法。在敏感性分析中使用了自相关稳健和经典方法。对个体Cq测定值分别与采集时间、严重程度和疫苗接种历史进行分析。结果 所选滞后关联在所有报告的指标家族中均为负值,滞后时间为1至9周。在奇瓦瓦州第二波中,Cq与DGE衍生的Rt之间的最强负相关出现在+8周(r = −0.745)。在哈利斯科州,出现在+7周(r = −0.464)。只有哈利斯科州支持完全调整的模型4(M4)。在那里,自举区间排除了零,对于实验室阳性病例和最终监测确认病例的Rt估计值,但未排除两个严重程度比例。排除最多四个终点周仍保留了两个Rt估计值的方向。在个体层面,Cq随着零、一、两剂记录的疫苗剂量而增加(每剂0.761个循环,95% CI 0.589至0.932),并且住院病例的Cq值比非住院病例低1.02个循环。结论 整体Cq在回顾性分析中与Rt表现出最一致的领先关联。回顾性关联发生在奇瓦瓦州第二波和哈利斯科州的数据校准滞后7–8周。相反,无泄漏的预测支持在一个分析单位中,中位数2周的预测范围内有逐步增加。因此,整体Cq的操作性预测范围尚未验证。如果前瞻性验证成功,每周整体Cq可能可以补充传统的麻疹监测,利用已经常规收集的实验室信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定通报的 麻疹 病例的每周中位实时逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 量化周期 (Cq) 值是否先于 发病率、传播 和 暴发 的严重性指标。
设计与对象
在 2025 年至 2026 年 麻疹 期间,对奇瓦瓦州和哈利斯科州单个墨西哥国家病例级 监测 提取物中记录的 10,910 个 Cq 测定结果和 11,735 个确诊的 麻疹 病例进行了回顾性二次分析。
核心方法
通过互相关、四个嵌套滞后回归模型 (M1–M4)、正式的 Toda–Yamamoto Granger 检验和二次校准滞后自回归模型检查了 发病率、传播 和严重性的十二周指标。移动块引导区间是回归推断的主要方法。敏感性分析中使用了自相关稳健的经典方法。
主要结果
在所有报告的指标系列中,选择滞后关联均为负值,领先时间为 1-9 周。Cq 和 DGE 衍生的 R t 之间最强的负相关发生在第二次吉娃娃波的 +8 周(r = -0.745)和哈利斯科州的 +7 周(r = -0.464)。只有哈利斯科州支持完全调整的型号 4 (M4)。
解释与创新
聚合 Cq 显示与 R t 最一致的回顾性领先关联。总体 Cq 的运营预测范围仍未得到验证。
科研与公卫意义
如果经过前瞻性验证,每周汇总 Cq 可以使用已经常规收集的实验室信息来补充传统的 麻疹 监测。
局限与证据强度
排除最多四个终点周可以保留两个 R t 估计值的方向。所提供的证据并未报告有关样本量、偏倚或普遍性的任何明确限制。

Population-level measles RT–PCR cycle threshold values and their relationships with disease severity, transmission, and incidence metrics: a time series analysis of the 2025–2026 outbreak in Mexico

Authors: Judith Carolina De Arcos-Jiménez, Ernestina Quintero-Salgado, Aurora Karina Robles-Martínez, José Luis Carrillo-Quintanar, Violeta Cassandra Vera-Cuevas, Pedro Martínez-Ayala, Christian Mendoza-Mújica, Gerardo F. Gómez-Abundis, Mauricio Alfredo Ambriz-Alarcón, Brian Rafael Rubio-Mora
Original abstract (English)
Objectives To determine whether the weekly median real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT–PCR) quantification cycle (Cq) value of notified measles cases precedes the incidence, transmission, and severity metrics of an outbreak. We focused particularly on the effective reproduction number ( R t ). We also assessed whether any association persisted after adjustment for a linear time trend, specimen composition, and the outcome's own past. Methods We conducted a retrospective secondary analysis of 10,910 Cq determinations and 11,735 confirmed cases recorded in a single Mexican national case-level surveillance extract for Chihuahua and Jalisco. Twelve reported weekly metrics of incidence, transmission, and severity were examined by cross-correlation, four nested lagged regression models (M1–M4), formal Toda–Yamamoto Granger tests, and a secondary calibrated-lag autoregressive model. Moving-block bootstrap intervals were the primary method for regression inference. Autocorrelation-robust and classical methods were used in sensitivity analyses. Individual Cq determinations were analyzed separately against collection timing, severity, and vaccination history. Results Selected-lag associations were negative across all reported metric families, at leads of 1–9 weeks. The strongest negative correlation between Cq and DGE-derived R t occurred at +8 weeks in the second Chihuahua wave ( r = −0.745). In Jalisco, it occurred at +7 weeks ( r = −0.464). Only Jalisco supported the fully adjusted model 4 (M4). There, bootstrap intervals excluded zero for both the laboratory-positive-case and final surveillance-confirmed-case R t estimates but not for the two severity proportions. Excluding up to four endpoint weeks preserved the direction of both R t estimates. At the individual level, Cq increased with zero, one, and two documented vaccine doses (0.761 cycles per dose, 95% CI 0.589 to 0.932) and was 1.02 cycles lower in hospitalized cases. Conclusions Aggregate Cq showed the most consistent retrospective leading association with R t . The retrospective associations occurred at data-calibrated lags of 7–8 weeks during the second Chihuahua wave and in Jalisco. Conversely, leak-free forecasting supported incremental gain in one analytic unit at a median 2-week horizon. The operational predictive horizon of aggregate Cq therefore remains unvalidated. If prospectively validated, weekly aggregate Cq could complement conventional measles surveillance using laboratory information that is already routinely collected.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine whether the weekly median real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT–PCR) quantification cycle (Cq) value of notified measles cases precedes the incidence, transmission, and severity metrics of an outbreak.
Design and population
A retrospective secondary analysis of 10,910 Cq determinations and 11,735 confirmed measles cases recorded in a single Mexican national case-level surveillance extract for Chihuahua and Jalisco was conducted during the 2025–2026 outbreak.
Core methods
Twelve weekly metrics of incidence, transmission, and severity were examined by cross-correlation, four nested lagged regression models (M1–M4), formal Toda–Yamamoto Granger tests, and a secondary calibrated-lag autoregressive model. Moving-block bootstrap intervals were the primary method for regression inference. Autocorrelation-robust and classical methods were used in sensitivity analyses.
Main results
Selected-lag associations were negative across all reported metric families, at leads of 1–9 weeks. The strongest negative correlation between Cq and DGE-derived R t occurred at +8 weeks in the second Chihuahua wave ( r = −0.745) and at +7 weeks in Jalisco ( r = −0.464). Only Jalisco supported the fully adjusted model 4 (M4).
Interpretation and novelty
Aggregate Cq showed the most consistent retrospective leading association with R t . The operational predictive horizon of aggregate Cq remains unvalidated.
Scientific and public-health significance
If prospectively validated, weekly aggregate Cq could complement conventional measles surveillance using laboratory information that is already routinely collected.
Limitations and evidence strength
Excluding up to four endpoint weeks preserved the direction of both R t estimates. The supplied evidence does not report any explicit limitations regarding sample size, bias, or generalisability.

溶瘤 麻疹病毒 在血管化间皮瘤芯片模型中重新编程肿瘤微环境

Oncolytic measles virus reprograms the tumor microenvironment in a vascularized mesothelioma-on-chip model
作者： Nikita Rajkumari, Mégane Willems, Judith Fresquet, Elise Douillard, Magali Devic, Frédéric Tangy, Hortense Perdrieau, Delphine Fradin, Jean-François Fonteneau, Nicolas Boisgerault
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 胸膜间皮瘤 (PM) 是一种罕见的侵袭性癌症,主要由石棉暴露引起。尽管双重免疫检查点抑制(抗 PD-1/抗 CTLA-4)现已被批准作为一线治疗,但临床获益仅限于一小部分患者,因此需要替代策略。溶瘤病毒代表了一种有前途的治疗方法,因为它们选择性地感染和裂解肿瘤细胞并表现出免疫原性。在 PM 中,我们发现 麻疹病毒 (MV) 的溶瘤活性主要依赖于 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 途径的改变。然而,迄今为止,尚未开发出体外模型来模拟复杂的肿瘤微环境 (TME),并准确评估 MV 对 PM 的疗效。方法我们使用模仿 TME 的微流控芯片设计了人性化 3D“芯片上血管化间皮瘤”(VMOC) 模型。VMOC 使用原代内皮细胞、肺成纤维细胞和患者来源的 PM 细胞产生。我们通过共聚焦成像、灌注测定和单细胞 RNA 测序来表征我们模型的功能。通过批量转录组学、多重 ELISA 和反卷积策略评估 MV 对 VMOC 的影响,并与 PM 患者的临床数据进行比较。结果我们首先证明了 VMOC 具有适当的内皮屏障完整性、可灌注性和血管对外部刺激的反应性的功能。我们证明了 VMOC 存在显着的异质性,存在不同的间充质、癌症和内皮亚群。有趣的是,将PM细胞添加到VMOC中既减少了来自TME的细胞中的IFN-I途径,也减少了以IFN-I信号传导特征为特征的新型内皮细胞亚群的丰度。通过内皮网络给予 MV 后,我们检测到 PM 细胞感染和死亡,以及 IFN-I 途径的强烈激活和多种炎症介质的产生。有趣的是,MV 在 VMOC 中诱导的蛋白质组和转录组炎症反应与用溶瘤病毒治疗的 PM 患者诱导的相似。结论 我们开发的 VMOC 模型能够对 MV 感染和 TME 重编程进行体外研究,为更深入地了解 TME 在 OV 反应中的作用以及开发提高临床疗效的新策略铺平道路。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
胸膜间皮瘤 (PM) 是一种罕见的侵袭性癌症,主要由石棉接触引起。尽管双重免疫检查点抑制(抗 PD-1/抗 CTLA-4)现已被批准作为一线治疗,但临床获益仅限于一小部分患者,因此需要替代策略。
设计与对象
我们使用模拟肿瘤微环境 (TME) 的微流控芯片设计了人性化 3D“芯片上血管化间皮瘤”(VMOC) 模型。VMOC 使用原代内皮细胞、肺成纤维细胞和患者来源的 PM 细胞产生。
核心方法
通过批量转录组学、多重 ELISA 和反卷积策略评估 麻疹病毒 (MV) 对 VMOC 的影响,并与 PM 患者的临床数据进行比较。我们通过共聚焦成像、灌注测定和单细胞 RNA 测序来表征我们模型的功能。
主要结果
在 PM 中,我们发现 麻疹病毒 (MV) 的溶瘤活性主要依赖于 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 途径的改变。我们首先证明了 VMOC 具有适当的内皮屏障完整性、可灌注性和血管对外部刺激的反应性的功能。
解释与创新
有趣的是,将PM细胞添加到VMOC中既减少了来自TME的细胞中的IFN-I途径,也减少了以IFN-I信号传导特征为特征的新型内皮细胞亚群的丰度。
科研与公卫意义
我们开发的 VMOC 模型能够对 MV 感染和 TME 重编程进行体外研究,为更深入地了解 TME 在 OV 反应中的作用以及开发提高临床疗效的新策略铺平道路。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Oncolytic measles virus reprograms the tumor microenvironment in a vascularized mesothelioma-on-chip model

Authors: Nikita Rajkumari, Mégane Willems, Judith Fresquet, Elise Douillard, Magali Devic, Frédéric Tangy, Hortense Perdrieau, Delphine Fradin, Jean-François Fonteneau, Nicolas Boisgerault
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Pleural mesothelioma (PM) is a rare and aggressive cancer primarily caused by asbestos exposure. Although dual immune checkpoint inhibition (anti-PD-1/anti-CTLA-4) is now approved as first-line therapy, clinical benefit is limited to a small subset of patients, necessitating the need for alternative strategies. Oncolytic viruses represent a promising therapeutic approach as they selectively infect and lyse tumor cells and display immunogenic properties. In PM, we showed that the oncolytic activity of measles virus (MV) is primarily dependent on alterations in the type I interferon (IFN-I) pathway. However, to date no in vitro models have yet been developed to mimic a sophisticated tumor microenvironment (TME), and accurately evaluate MV efficacy in PM. Methods We designed a humanized 3D “vascularized mesothelioma-on-chip” (VMOC) model using microfluidic chips that mimic a TME. VMOC are generated using primary endothelial cells, lung fibroblasts and patient-derived PM cells. We characterized the functionality of our model by confocal imaging, perfusion assay and single-cell RNA sequencing. The impact of MV on the VMOC was assessed by bulk transcriptomics, multiplex ELISA and deconvolution strategies, and compared to clinical data in patients with PM. Results We first proved VMOC functionality with an appropriate endothelial barrier integrity, perfusability, and vascular responsiveness to external stimuli. We demonstrated significant heterogeneity in the VMOC, with the presence of different mesenchymal, cancer and endothelial subpopulations. Interestingly, the addition of PM cells to the VMOC reduced both the IFN-I pathway in cells from the TME, and the abundance of a novel endothelial cell subset characterized by an IFN-I signaling signature. Following MV administration via the endothelial network, we detected PM cell infection and death, as well as strong activation of the IFN-I pathway with the production of multiple inflammatory mediators. Interestingly, the proteomic and transcriptomic inflammatory response induced by MV in VMOCs was similar to that induced in PM patients treated with an oncolytic virus. Conclusions The VMOC model we developed enables in vitro investigation of MV infection and TME reprogramming, paving the way for a deeper understanding of the TME role in OV responses and the development of novel strategies to improve clinical efficacy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pleural mesothelioma (PM) is a rare and aggressive cancer primarily caused by asbestos exposure. Although dual immune checkpoint inhibition (anti-PD-1/anti-CTLA-4) is now approved as first-line therapy, clinical benefit is limited to a small subset of patients, necessitating the need for alternative strategies.
Design and population

Core methods
The impact of measles virus (MV) on the VMOC was assessed by bulk transcriptomics, multiplex ELISA and deconvolution strategies, and compared to clinical data in patients with PM. We characterized the functionality of our model by confocal imaging, perfusion assay and single-cell RNA sequencing.
Main results
In PM, we showed that the oncolytic activity of measles virus (MV) is primarily dependent on alterations in the type I interferon (IFN-I) pathway. We first proved VMOC functionality with an appropriate endothelial barrier integrity, perfusability, and vascular responsiveness to external stimuli.
Interpretation and novelty
Interestingly, the addition of PM cells to the VMOC reduced both the IFN-I pathway in cells from the TME, and the abundance of a novel endothelial cell subset characterized by an IFN-I signaling signature.
Scientific and public-health significance
The VMOC model we developed enables in vitro investigation of MV infection and TME reprogramming, paving the way for a deeper understanding of the TME role in OV responses and the development of novel strategies to improve clinical efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

麻疹(风疹)病毒定性实时逆转录聚合酶链反应测定:自动化工作流程的开发和临床验证

Measles ( rubeola) virus qualitative real-time reverse transcription–polymerase chain reaction assay: development and clinical validation on an automated workflow
作者： Lan Yu, Li Chen, Kevin Dumas, Nickolas Weyandt, Michael Meyers, Pei Irwin, Jennifer L Eitson, John W Schoggins, Jason Y Park
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 麻疹病毒 (MeV) RNA 检测是确诊感染的重要方法。本研究描述了用于 MeV 检测的定性实时逆转录酶聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 测定的临床验证。方法 使用先前文献中报道的引物和探针开发并验证了实时 RT-PCR 测定法。使用参考材料、培养的病毒和之前测试的临床咽拭子样本对该检测进行了评估。使用机器人平台和实时 PCR 仪器建立了 RNA 提取、RT-PCR 扩增和结果报告的自动化工作流程。该检测以 麻疹 核蛋白 (N) 和血凝素 (H) 基因为目标,并使用人 RNase P 基因作为内部对照。结果 MeV RT-PCR 检测的检测限为每毫升 500 个 基因组 拷贝。该测定针对 85 个不同的样品进行了测试:50 个阳性临床样品和参考材料以及 35 个阴性临床样品或参考材料。所有预期结果均得到正确识别,灵敏度 (50/50) 和特异性 (35/35) 均为 100%。该测定显示 100% 测定间和测定内重现性。未观察到与其他呼吸道病毒或常见细菌和真菌病原体的交叉反应。讨论 这种自动化 麻疹 RT-PCR 测定显示出高精度、灵敏度、特异性和重现性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发和验证用于检测 麻疹病毒 (MeV) RNA 的定性实时 RT-PCR 检测,因为 MeV RNA 检测对于确认感染至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究描述了使用参考材料、培养病毒和之前测试的临床咽拭子样本对 MeV 检测定性实时 RT-PCR 测定进行的临床验证。
核心方法
使用先前文献中报道的引物和探针开发并验证了实时 RT-PCR 测定。该检测以 麻疹 核蛋白 (N) 和血凝素 (H) 基因为目标,并使用人 RNase P 基因作为内部对照。使用机器人平台和实时 PCR 仪器建立了 RNA 提取、RT-PCR 扩增和结果报告的自动化工作流程。
主要结果
MeV RT-PCR 检测的检测限为每毫升 500 个 基因组 拷贝。该测定针对 85 个不同的样品进行了测试:50 个阳性临床样品和参考材料以及 35 个阴性临床样品或参考材料。该测定显示 100% 测定间和测定内重现性。
解释与创新
自动化 麻疹 RT-PCR 检测具有较高的准确性、灵敏度、特异性和重现性,是提高 MeV 检测效率和可靠性的一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效、更可靠的 麻疹 诊断方法提供信息,这对于 监测 和 暴发 的反应至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告局限性,但结果是基于对参考材料和临床样本的综合评估,对检测的性能给出了适度的信心。

Measles ( rubeola) virus qualitative real-time reverse transcription–polymerase chain reaction assay: development and clinical validation on an automated workflow

Authors: Lan Yu, Li Chen, Kevin Dumas, Nickolas Weyandt, Michael Meyers, Pei Irwin, Jennifer L Eitson, John W Schoggins, Jason Y Park
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Detection of Measles virus (MeV) RNA is an important method for confirming infection. This study described the clinical validation of a qualitative real-time reverse transcriptase–polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) assay for MeV detection. Methods A real-time RT-PCR assay was developed and validated using primers and probes previously reported in the literature. The assay was evaluated using reference materials, cultured virus, and previously tested clinical throat swab samples. An automated workflow was established for RNA extraction, RT-PCR amplification, and result reporting using robotic platforms and a real-time PCR instrument. The assay targets the measles nucleoprotein (N) and hemagglutinin (H) genes, with human RNase P gene used as an internal control. Results The MeV RT-PCR assay has a limit of detection of 500 genome copies per milliliter. The assay was tested against 85 different samples: 50 positive clinical samples and reference materials and 35 negative clinical samples or reference materials. All expected results were correctly identified, giving 100% sensitivity (50/50) and specificity (35/35). The assay showed 100% interassay and intra-assay reproducibility. No cross-reactivity was observed with other respiratory viruses or common bacterial and fungal pathogens. Discussion This automated measles RT-PCR assay demonstrated high accuracy, sensitivity, specificity, and reproducibility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop and validate a qualitative real-time RT-PCR assay for the detection of Measles virus (MeV) RNA, as MeV RNA detection is crucial for confirming infection.
Design and population
This study described the clinical validation of a qualitative real-time RT-PCR assay for MeV detection, using reference materials, cultured virus, and previously tested clinical throat swab samples.
Core methods
A real-time RT-PCR assay was developed and validated using primers and probes previously reported in the literature. The assay targets the measles nucleoprotein (N) and hemagglutinin (H) genes, with human RNase P gene used as an internal control. An automated workflow was established for RNA extraction, RT-PCR amplification, and result reporting using robotic platforms and a real-time PCR instrument.
Main results
The MeV RT-PCR assay has a limit of detection of 500 genome copies per milliliter. The assay was tested against 85 different samples: 50 positive clinical samples and reference materials and 35 negative clinical samples or reference materials. The assay showed 100% interassay and intra-assay reproducibility.
Interpretation and novelty
The automated measles RT-PCR assay demonstrated high accuracy, sensitivity, specificity, and reproducibility, which is a novel approach for improving the efficiency and reliability of MeV detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more efficient and reliable diagnostic methods for measles, which is critical for surveillance and outbreak response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not report limitations, but the results are based on a comprehensive evaluation of reference materials and clinical samples, giving a moderate confidence in the assay's performance.

布基纳法索 2023 年至 2024 年 麻疹 的流行病学、临床和临床病程概况

Epidemiological, Clinical, and Clinical Course Profile of Measles during the 2023–2024 Outbreak in Burkina Faso
作者： Zemane Guelilou, Konaté Issa, Gnamou Arouna, Ouedraogo Gafourou Arsène, Ouedraogo Dramane, Sanfo Salmata, Diallo Ismael, Kafando Lassané, Barry Boubacar, Arouna Gnamou
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 是一种由麻疹病毒属副粘病毒引起的广泛发疹性发热性疾病。它是最具传染性的传染病之一。
设计与对象
This descriptive cross-sectional study used data on measles cases reported in Burkina Faso from 1 November 2023 to 31 August 2024. The Hauts-Bassins and Centre regions were the most affected, with incidence rates of 67.78 and 60.17 cases per 100,000 inhabitants, respectively.
核心方法
数据来自人口健康保护局的数据库。使用 Excel 2019 进行描述性分析。
主要结果
A total of 7,149 measles cases were included. The overall incidence of measles was 33.28 per 100,000 inhabitants. Males were overrepresented, accounting for 51.45% of cases. The median age was 4 years. The most frequently represented age group was 0–5 years, accounting for 55.81% of cases. Most measles cases were unvaccinated, accounting for 55.31% of cases. The health districts of Houndé (14.69%) in the Hauts-Bassins region and Bogodogo (10.77%) in the Centre region reported the highest numbers of cases. Epidemiological weeks S08-2024 and S12-2024 recorded the highest numbers of measles cases, accounting for 7.33% and 7.32% of cases, respectively. Only 11.87% of cases had travelled within the seven days before the onset of clinical symptoms.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了 麻疹 持续的公共卫生负担,其中男性和未接种疫苗的个体所占比例明显过高。该研究提供了 2023 年至 2024 年 暴发 期间该疾病的流行病学和临床特征的详细概况。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为布基纳法索的有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略和 暴发 应对措施提供信息,特别是在疾病负担最高的上盆地和中部地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于报告的病例数据,不包括大多数病例的实验室确认,这可能会引入偏差。由于依赖描述性分析且缺乏详细的统计方法,证据强度中等。

Epidemiological, Clinical, and Clinical Course Profile of Measles during the 2023–2024 Outbreak in Burkina Faso

Authors: Zemane Guelilou, Konaté Issa, Gnamou Arouna, Ouedraogo Gafourou Arsène, Ouedraogo Dramane, Sanfo Salmata, Diallo Ismael, Kafando Lassané, Barry Boubacar, Arouna Gnamou
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Measles is a widespread exanthematous febrile illness caused by a paramyxovirus of the genus Morbillivirus. The reservoir is exclusively human, and transmission occurs between humans. It is one of the most contagious infectious diseases. In Burkina Faso, measles remains a public health problem. The country experienced a major epidemic from November 2023 to August 2024. Objective: To study the epidemiological, clinical, and disease-progression profile of measles during the 2023–2024 epidemic in Burkina Faso. Methods: This descriptive cross-sectional study used data on measles cases reported in Burkina Faso from 1 November 2023 to 31 August 2024. The data were obtained from the database of the Directorate of Population Health Protection. Descriptive analysis was performed using Excel 2019. Results: A total of 7,149 measles cases were included. The overall incidence of measles was 33.28 per 100,000 inhabitants. Males were overrepresented, accounting for 51.45% of cases. The median age was 4 years. The most frequently represented age group was 0–5 years, accounting for 55.81% of cases. Most measles cases were unvaccinated, accounting for 55.31% of cases. The Hauts-Bassins and Centre regions were the most affected, with incidence rates of 67.78 and 60.17 cases per 100,000 inhabitants, respectively. The health districts of Houndé (14.69%) in the Hauts-Bassins region and Bogodogo (10.77%) in the Centre region reported the highest numbers of cases. Epidemiological weeks S08-2024 and S12-2024 recorded the highest numbers of measles cases, accounting for 7.33% and 7.32% of cases, respectively. Only 11.87% of cases had travelled within the seven days before the onset of clinical symptoms. Fever, rash, and cough were the most commonly reported symptoms, occurring in 96.87%, 95.06%, and 89.29% of cases, respectively. Laboratory confirmation was obtained in 9.79% of cases. The measles case-fatality rate during the outbreak was 0.45%. Conclusion: Measles remains a public health problem, underscoring the need to strengthen the Expanded Programme on Immunization, outbreak response, and nationwide vaccination strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles is a widespread exanthematous febrile illness caused by a paramyxovirus of the genus Morbillivirus. It is one of the most contagious infectious diseases.
Design and population
This descriptive cross-sectional study used data on measles cases reported in Burkina Faso from 1 November 2023 to 31 August 2024. The Hauts-Bassins and Centre regions were the most affected, with incidence rates of 67.78 and 60.17 cases per 100,000 inhabitants, respectively.
Core methods
The data were obtained from the database of the Directorate of Population Health Protection. Descriptive analysis was performed using Excel 2019.
Main results
A total of 7,149 measles cases were included. The overall incidence of measles was 33.28 per 100,000 inhabitants. Males were overrepresented, accounting for 51.45% of cases. The median age was 4 years. The most frequently represented age group was 0–5 years, accounting for 55.81% of cases. Most measles cases were unvaccinated, accounting for 55.31% of cases. The health districts of Houndé (14.69%) in the Hauts-Bassins region and Bogodogo (10.77%) in the Centre region reported the highest numbers of cases. Epidemiological weeks S08-2024 and S12-2024 recorded the highest numbers of measles cases, accounting for 7.33% and 7.32% of cases, respectively. Only 11.87% of cases had travelled within the seven days before the onset of clinical symptoms.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the continued public health burden of measles, with a notable overrepresentation of males and unvaccinated individuals. The study provides a detailed profile of the disease's epidemiological and clinical characteristics during the 2023–2024 outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted vaccination strategies and outbreak response measures in Burkina Faso, particularly in the Hauts-Bassins and Centre regions where the disease burden was highest.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on reported case data and does not include laboratory confirmation for most cases, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to reliance on descriptive analysis and the absence of detailed statistical methods.

具有不完整 疫苗接种 和 1 个免疫力减弱的 麻疹 的 SVIR 模型分析:Sidoarjo-Indonesia 案例

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case
作者： Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
摘要中文翻译麻疹仍然是印度尼西亚的重大公共卫生挑战,尤其是在疫苗接种覆盖率异质性和免疫力下降的地区。本研究开发并分析了一个针对麻疹传播的易感-接种-感染-康复(SVIR)分组模型,该模型纳入了免疫力下降的因素。基本再生数R0是通过下一代矩阵方法推导的,并且通过李雅普诺夫函数严格证明了无病平衡态和地方性平衡态的全局稳定性。模型参数是从2023年Sidoarjo市每周的监测数据中使用粒子群优化(PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)进行评估。PSO方法实现了最低的MAPE(11.17%),优于其他方法。估计的R0 = 0.668确认了无病状态。归一化敏感性分析确定疫苗效力是最关键的参数;疫苗效力下降至0 .75会使R0接近临界阈值,任何进一步的减少都会使系统进入地方性状态。这些发现强调了通过加强剂接种计划和强化疫苗接种策略保持高疫苗效力的必要性,以在区级维持麻疹的消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本繁殖数R0,并通过Lyapunov函数严格证明了无病平衡和地方病平衡的全局稳定性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本研究开发并分析了印度尼西亚诗都阿佐市麻疹 传播 的易感-疫苗接种-感染-康复 (SVIR) 区室模型,其中考虑了免疫力的减弱。
主要结果
模型参数是根据西多阿佐市 2023 年每周的 监测 数据使用粒子群优化 (PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波器、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 进行评估。PSO 实现了最低的 MAPE (11.17%),优于其他方法。
解释与创新
估计的 R0 = 0.668 确认无病状态。
科研与公卫意义
麻疹 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖范围不均匀且免疫力下降的地区。
局限与证据强度
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case

Authors: Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity. This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity. The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions. Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods. The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime. Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions.

The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.

麻疹 传播的最优控制及成本效益分析

Optimal Control and Cost-Effectiveness Analysis of Measles Transmission
作者： Moustafa El-Shahed, Raseel Al-Qubaysi, Yousef Alnafisah
摘要中文翻译本研究开发并分析了 麻疹 传播 的确定性隔室模型,该模型包含两剂量 疫苗接种、不完善的剂量特异性 疫苗 保护、突破性感染和非线性饱和 发病率。人群分为易感人群、第一剂疫苗接种者、第二剂疫苗接种者、暴露者、感染者和康复者。建立了模型的基本数学属性,包括解的正性和有界性。对无病和地方病平衡进行了表征,并使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本再生数 R0。当 R0 1 模型承认独特的地方性平衡时,无病平衡是全局渐近稳定的,该平衡相对于生物学可行区域的内部是全局渐近稳定的。分析灵敏度分析确定了模型参数对 R0 的局部影响。特别是,饱和度参数没有进入R0,但数值分析表明它可以显着影响地方性感染水平。该模型进一步扩展到具有三个时间依赖性无量纲控制的最优控制框架,这三个控制代表强化的第一剂量 疫苗接种、强化的第二剂量 疫苗接种 和增强的感染病例清除。最优控制的存在性成立,并使用庞特里亚金极大值原理推导出必要的最优性条件。使用前向-后向扫描方法对所得状态伴随系统进行数值求解。数值模拟表明,组合的最佳干预措施减少了有限控制范围内的暴露疾病和传染病负担,而敏感性分析表明,最佳控制概况取决于分配给疾病负担和干预努力的相对权重。随后使用基于模型的平均成本效益比(ACER)和增量成本效益比(ICER)措施对七种干预组合进行比较。在基线加权方案下,单独使用第一剂 疫苗接种 可以避免每个病例的加权干预工作量最小。考虑到强大和扩展的优势后,增量边界由单独的第一剂 疫苗接种、第一剂 疫苗接种 与感染病例清除相结合以及完整的三控制策略组成。沿着这一前沿增加流行病学益处伴随着增量加权干预努力的增加。这些结果说明了如何将剂量特异性 疫苗接种、非线性 传播 和感染病例干预整合到一个统一的框架中,以比较 麻疹 控制策略,同时明确考虑干预工作的假设。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究开发并分析了 麻疹 传播 的确定性隔室模型,该模型包含两剂量 疫苗接种、不完善的剂量特异性 疫苗 保护、突破性感染和非线性饱和 发病率。该研究旨在评估 麻疹 疾病的最佳控制策略。
设计与对象
人群分为易感人群、第一剂疫苗接种者、第二剂疫苗接种者、暴露者、感染者和康复者。该研究是使用数学建模方法来模拟 麻疹 传播 动力学进行的。
核心方法
建立了模型的基本数学属性,包括解的正性和有界性。对无病和地方病平衡进行了表征,并使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本再生数 R0。该模型进一步扩展到具有三个时间依赖性无量纲控制的最优控制框架,这三个控制代表强化的第一剂量 疫苗接种、强化的第二剂量 疫苗接种 和增强的感染病例清除。
主要结果
当 R0 < 1 时,无病平衡是全局渐近稳定的,并且该模型承认独特的地方性平衡,相对于生物学可行区域的内部来说,该平衡是全局渐近稳定的。数值模拟表明,组合的最佳干预措施可以减少有限控制范围内的暴露疾病和传染病负担。
解释与创新
结果说明了如何将剂量特异性 疫苗接种、非线性 传播 和感染病例干预整合到一个统一的框架中,以比较 麻疹 控制策略,同时明确考虑干预工作的假设。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过强调特定剂量 麻疹 和感染病例干预措施整合的重要性,为制定具有成本效益的 麻疹 控制策略提供信息。它们为评估不同 疫苗接种 方案对疾病 传播 的影响提供了基础。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型,并未考虑现实世界的复杂性,例如行为变化或空间异质性。证据来自理论分析和数值模拟,可能无法完全捕捉生物变异性。

Optimal Control and Cost-Effectiveness Analysis of Measles Transmission

Authors: Moustafa El-Shahed, Raseel Al-Qubaysi, Yousef Alnafisah
Original abstract (English)
This study develops and analyzes a deterministic compartmental model for measles transmission incorporating two-dose vaccination, imperfect dose-specific vaccine protection, breakthrough infection, and nonlinear saturated incidence. The population is divided into susceptible, first-dose vaccinated, second-dose vaccinated, exposed, infectious, and recovered classes. The fundamental mathematical properties of the model are established, including positivity and boundedness of solutions. The disease-free and endemic equilibria are characterized, and the basic reproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix approach. The disease-free equilibrium is globally asymptotically stable when R0 1 the model admits a unique endemic equilibrium that is globally asymptotically stable relative to the interior of the biologically feasible region. Analytical sensitivity analysis identifies the local influence of the model parameters on R0. In particular, the saturation parameter does not enter R0, but numerical analysis shows that it can substantially affect the endemic infection level. The model is further extended to an optimal-control framework with three time-dependent, dimensionless controls representing intensified first-dose vaccination, intensified second-dose vaccination, and enhanced infectious-case removal. The existence of an optimal control is established, and Pontryagin’s Maximum Principle is used to derive the necessary optimality conditions. The resulting state–adjoint system is solved numerically using the Forward–Backward Sweep Method. Numerical simulations show that the combined optimal intervention reduces the exposed and infectious disease burdens over the finite control horizon, while sensitivity analysis demonstrates that the optimal-control profiles depend on the relative weights assigned to disease burden and intervention effort. Seven intervention combinations are subsequently compared using the model-based Average Cost-Effectiveness Ratio (ACER) and Incremental Cost-Effectiveness Ratio (ICER) measures. Under the baseline weighting scheme, first-dose vaccination alone has the smallest weighted intervention effort per case averted. After strong and extended dominance are accounted for, the incremental frontier consists of first-dose vaccination alone, first-dose vaccination combined with infectious-case removal, and the full three-control strategy. Increasing epidemiological benefit along this frontier is accompanied by increasing incremental weighted intervention effort. These results illustrate how dose-specific vaccination, nonlinear transmission, and infectious-case intervention can be integrated within a unified framework for comparing measles-control strategies while explicitly accounting for the assumptions underlying intervention effort.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study develops and analyzes a deterministic compartmental model for measles transmission incorporating two-dose vaccination, imperfect dose-specific vaccine protection, breakthrough infection, and nonlinear saturated incidence. The research aims to evaluate optimal control strategies for measles disease.
Design and population
The population is divided into susceptible, first-dose vaccinated, second-dose vaccinated, exposed, infectious, and recovered classes. The study is conducted using a mathematical modeling approach to simulate measles transmission dynamics.
Core methods
The fundamental mathematical properties of the model are established, including positivity and boundedness of solutions. The disease-free and endemic equilibria are characterized, and the basic reproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix approach. The model is further extended to an optimal-control framework with three time-dependent, dimensionless controls representing intensified first-dose vaccination, intensified second-dose vaccination, and enhanced infectious-case removal.
Main results
The disease-free equilibrium is globally asymptotically stable when R0 < 1, and the model admits a unique endemic equilibrium that is globally asymptotically stable relative to the interior of the biologically feasible region. Numerical simulations show that the combined optimal intervention reduces the exposed and infectious disease burdens over the finite control horizon.
Interpretation and novelty
The results illustrate how dose-specific vaccination, nonlinear transmission, and infectious-case intervention can be integrated within a unified framework for comparing measles-control strategies while explicitly accounting for the assumptions underlying intervention effort.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform the development of cost-effective measles-control strategies by highlighting the importance of dose-specific vaccination and the integration of infectious-case interventions. They provide a basis for evaluating the impact of different vaccination protocols on disease transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mathematical model and does not account for real-world complexities such as behavioral changes or spatial heterogeneity. The evidence is derived from theoretical analysis and numerical simulations, which may not fully capture biological variability.

德克萨斯州学区的 麻疹 暴发 风险:建模研究。

Risk of Measles Outbreak in Texas School Districts: A Modeling Study.
作者： Martial L Ndeffo-Mbah, Sina Mokhtar
摘要中文翻译我们为德克萨斯州学区级别的 K-12 公立学校开发了 麻疹 传播 模型。我们发现,2025 年至 2030 年间,德克萨斯州西部地区的 麻疹 麻疹 风险最高,而大都市区的地区可能会出现 麻疹 病例数量最多的地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估德克萨斯州学区的 麻疹 暴发 风险,并了解导致该风险的因素。
设计与对象
进行了一项建模研究,以评估 2025 年至 2030 年间德克萨斯州学区 K-12 公立学校的 麻疹 传播 动态。
核心方法
研究人员开发了学区级别的 麻疹 传播 模型,以根据人口统计和 疫苗接种 数据模拟 暴发 风险和病例数。
主要结果
结果表明,德克萨斯州西部的地区面临 麻疹 暴发 的风险最大,而大都市区可能会在 麻疹 期间经历最多的病例数。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为强调了 麻疹 麻疹 风险的区域差异,这可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以帮助指导德克萨斯州学区的区域 疫苗接种 战略和 暴发 准备工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于建模,可能无法完全捕捉现实世界的复杂性,并且由于依赖模拟数据,证据并不充分。

Risk of Measles Outbreak in Texas School Districts: A Modeling Study.

Authors: Martial L Ndeffo-Mbah, Sina Mokhtar
Original abstract (English)
We developed a measles transmission model for K-12 public schools in Texas at the school-district-level. We found that, between 2025 and 2030, districts in West Texas are most at risk for measles outbreaks, while districts in large metropolitan areas would likely see the largest number of cases in an outbreak.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the risk of measles outbreaks in Texas school districts and understand the factors contributing to this risk.
Design and population
A modeling study was conducted to evaluate measles transmission dynamics in K-12 public schools across Texas school districts between 2025 and 2030.
Core methods
The researchers developed a measles transmission model at the school-district level to simulate outbreak risks and case numbers based on demographic and vaccination data.
Main results
The results indicated that districts in West Texas are most at risk for measles outbreaks, while large metropolitan areas would likely experience the highest number of cases during an outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as highlighting regional disparities in measles outbreak risk, which could inform targeted public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can help guide regional vaccination strategies and outbreak preparedness in Texas school districts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on modeling, which may not fully capture real-world complexities, and the evidence is moderate due to the reliance on simulated data.

年龄分层数学模型,用于告知最佳 麻疹 疫苗接种 策略

An age-stratified mathematical model to inform optimal measles vaccination strategies
作者： Samiran Ghosh, Indrajit Ghosh, Siuli Mukhopadhyay
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管存在有效的 麻疹,但 麻疹 仍然是一个重大的全球公共卫生威胁。世卫组织建议在 9-12 个月龄时首次注射 麻疹 疫苗,以平衡母体抗体干扰和免疫系统成熟度。然而,9 个月以下的婴儿仍然非常脆弱,特别是在 传播 高且群体免疫力低的地区。方法:本研究引入了 麻疹 传播 的年龄分层、多室模型,该模型通过明确模拟母体免疫力衰退和早期感染风险来捕获未接种疫苗的婴儿的感染。我们证明了模型的正性并推导出瞬时替换数的解析表达式。对于非年龄分层版本,我们分析平衡点及其稳定性。我们还对模型参数相对于 30 年来 麻疹 的总和 发病率 进行了全局敏感性分析。此外,使用引导算法,通过将模型拟合到六个 麻疹 流行国家的年度 发病率 数据来确定关键参数和特定年龄的 传播 率。结果:1-5岁儿童中的传播与累积麻疹 发病率具有最强的正相关性,而MCV1疗效和覆盖率具有最强的负相关性。2020 年至 2023 年期间估计瞬时更换数量在 0.85 至 1.15 之间波动。在模型假设下,包含 MCV0 的场景比不包含 MCV0 的场景产生的预计 麻疹 发病率 的减少幅度更大。所提出的模型提供了一个用于评估假设的 疫苗接种 场景的框架,包括不同的常规和补充剂量方案。结论:该研究估计了避免病例的相对数量,并预测了几种假设情况下的消除时间表,强调将常规免疫和补充免疫与早期 麻疹 疫苗接种 等替代假设相结合可能非常有效。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管存在有效的 疫苗,但 麻疹 仍然是一个重大的全球公共卫生威胁。世卫组织建议在 9-12 个月龄时首次注射 麻疹 疫苗,以平衡母体抗体干扰和免疫系统成熟度。然而,9 个月以下的婴儿仍然非常脆弱,特别是在 传播 高且群体免疫力低的地区。
设计与对象
本研究引入了 麻疹 传播 的年龄分层、多室模型,该模型通过明确模拟母体免疫力衰退和早期感染风险来捕获未接种疫苗的婴儿的感染情况。
核心方法
该研究使用年龄分层、多室方法对 麻疹 传播 进行建模,分析平衡点及其稳定性,并对模型参数进行全局敏感性分析。该模型使用引导算法拟合来自六个 麻疹 流行国家的年度 发病率 数据。
主要结果
1-5 岁儿童中的 传播 与累积 麻疹 发病率 具有最强的正相关性,而 MCV1 疗效和覆盖率具有最强的负相关性。2020 年至 2023 年期间估计瞬时更换数量在 0.85 至 1.15 之间波动。在模型假设下,包含 MCV0 的场景比不包含 MCV0 的场景产生的预计 麻疹 发病率 的减少幅度更大。
解释与创新
该研究估计了避免病例的相对数量,并预测了几种假设情况下的消除时间表,强调将常规免疫和补充免疫与早期 麻疹 疫苗接种 等替代假设相结合可能非常有效。
科研与公卫意义
所提出的模型提供了一个用于评估假设的 疫苗接种 场景的框架,包括不同的常规和补充剂量方案,并且可以告知最佳的 麻疹 疫苗接种 策略,以减少 发病率 并实现消除。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于模型假设以及模型参数与六个国家数据的拟合,这可能无法推广到所有地区。证据强度中等。

An age-stratified mathematical model to inform optimal measles vaccination strategies

Authors: Samiran Ghosh, Indrajit Ghosh, Siuli Mukhopadhyay
Original abstract (English)
Background: Measles remains a significant global public health threat despite the availability of an effective vaccine. WHO recommends the first measles vaccine dose at 9–12 months of age, balancing maternal antibody interference and immune system maturity. However, infants younger than 9 months remain highly vulnerable, especially in regions with high transmission and low herd immunity. Methods: This study introduces an age-stratified, multi-compartmental model of measles transmission that captures infections in unvaccinated infants by explicitly modelling maternal immunity decay and early infection risks. We prove the model’s positivity and derive an analytical expression for the instantaneous replacement number. For a non-age-stratified version, we analyze equilibrium points and their stability. We also conducted a global sensitivity analysis of the model parameters with respect to the aggregate incidence of measles over 30 years. Further, using a bootstrap algorithm, critical parameters and age-specific transmission rates are identified by fitting the model to yearly incidence data from six measles-prevalent countries. Results: Transmission among children aged 1–5 years had the strongest positive association with cumulative measles incidence, whereas MCV1 efficacy and coverage had the strongest negative associations. Estimated instantaneous replacement numbers fluctuated between 0.85 and 1.15 during 2020–2023. Under the model assumptions, scenarios including MCV0 produced larger reductions in projected measles incidence than those without MCV0. The proposed model provides a framework for evaluating hypothetical vaccination scenarios, including different routine and supplementary dose schedules. Conclusions: The study estimates the relative number of cases averted and projects elimination timelines for several hypothetical scenarios, highlighting that combining routine and supplementary immunizations with alternative assumptions such as early measles vaccination may be highly effective.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a significant global public health threat despite the availability of an effective vaccine. WHO recommends the first measles vaccine dose at 9–12 months of age, balancing maternal antibody interference and immune system maturity. However, infants younger than 9 months remain highly vulnerable, especially in regions with high transmission and low herd immunity.
Design and population
This study introduces an age-stratified, multi-compartmental model of measles transmission that captures infections in unvaccinated infants by explicitly modelling maternal immunity decay and early infection risks.
Core methods
The study models measles transmission using an age-stratified, multi-compartmental approach, analyzing equilibrium points and their stability, and conducting a global sensitivity analysis of model parameters. The model is fitted to yearly incidence data from six measles-prevalent countries using a bootstrap algorithm.
Main results
Transmission among children aged 1–5 years had the strongest positive association with cumulative measles incidence, whereas MCV1 efficacy and coverage had the strongest negative associations. Estimated instantaneous replacement numbers fluctuated between 0.85 and 1.15 during 2020–2023. Under the model assumptions, scenarios including MCV0 produced larger reductions in projected measles incidence than those without MCV0.
Interpretation and novelty
The study estimates the relative number of cases averted and projects elimination timelines for several hypothetical scenarios, highlighting that combining routine and supplementary immunizations with alternative assumptions such as early measles vaccination may be highly effective.
Scientific and public-health significance
The proposed model provides a framework for evaluating hypothetical vaccination scenarios, including different routine and supplementary dose schedules, and can inform optimal measles vaccination strategies to reduce incidence and achieve elimination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on model assumptions and the fitting of model parameters to data from six countries, which may not generalize to all regions. The evidence strength is moderate.

通过高通量测序对 麻疹、风疹和腮腺炎病毒生产株进行遗传表征

Genetic characterization of production strains of measles, rubella, and mumps viruses by high-throughput sequencing
作者： A. I. Zheltukhina, A. B. Komissarov, A. V. Fadeev, N. D. Yolshin, M. M. Pisareva, V. A. Eder, M. Korzhanova, A. D. Ksenafontov, A. A. Perederiy, O. V. Ohlopkova
摘要中文翻译自发的 突变 经常在含 RNA 病毒的 基因组 中产生和积累,例如 麻疹、风疹和腮腺炎。生产含有活 疫苗 病毒的 疫苗 产品的现代要求建议控制生产菌株的遗传稳定性。高通量测序方法可以确定病毒群体中出现的所有 突变,并评估生产菌株的遗传稳定性。对麻疹(Leningrad-16)、风疹(RA27/3)和流行性腮腺炎(Leningrad-3)病毒种子株和生产株基因组的核苷酸序列进行分析,并与疫苗株基因组的参考核苷酸序列(在Gen-Bank数据库中提交)进行比较。材料和方法。本研究使用疫苗生产菌株(麻疹培养活体、风疹培养活体和流行性腮腺炎培养活体)。使用 MGI DNBSEQ G-400 和 Illumina NextSeq2000 遗传分析仪进行整个 基因组 测序。在处理和分析测序数据时,使用了以下程序和工具:FastQC v.0.12.1、FastP v.0.23.4、BWA-MEM2 v.2.2.1、VizCoV 工具。对研究样本中病毒群体内的单核苷酸多态性 (SNP) 的分析揭示了许多微小变异,这些变异在进一步积累后可能导致 疫苗 毒株蛋白的共有序列发生变化。对于所研究的麻疹病毒样本,在主要麻疹病毒抗原H蛋白中未发现SNP,然而,在F蛋白中检测到非同义多态性,该多态性出现在病毒基因组的30.0-40.9%中。SNP 在风疹病毒的衣壳蛋白 C 和表面糖蛋白 E2 中被发现,在病毒群体中出现的频率高达 30%。在腮腺炎病毒的表面抗原血凝素神经氨酸酶 HN 和融合蛋白 F 中发现了 SNP,出现在不到 20% 病毒群体的 基因组 中。生产菌株基因组中未检出影响麻疹、风疹、流行性腮腺炎疫苗免疫原性的突变。已鉴定出的一组微小变异在病毒群体中出现频率超过10%,随着进一步积累,可导致疫苗毒株共有序列发生变化,使得利用高通量测序监测疫苗毒株稳定性具有相关性。

自发的 突变 通常在含 RNA 病毒的 基因组 中产生并积累,例如 麻疹、风疹和腮腺炎。生产含有活 疫苗 病毒的 疫苗 产品的现代要求建议控制生产菌株的遗传稳定性。

本研究使用疫苗生产菌株(麻疹培养活体、风疹培养活体和流行性腮腺炎培养活体)。高通量测序方法可以确定病毒群体中出现的所有 突变,并评估生产菌株的遗传稳定性。

对麻疹(Leningrad-16)、风疹(RA27/3)和流行性腮腺炎(Leningrad-3)病毒种子株和生产株基因组的核苷酸序列进行分析,并与疫苗株基因组的参考核苷酸序列(在Gen-Bank数据库中提交)进行比较。

Genetic characterization of production strains of measles, rubella, and mumps viruses by high-throughput sequencing

Authors: A. I. Zheltukhina, A. B. Komissarov, A. V. Fadeev, N. D. Yolshin, M. M. Pisareva, V. A. Eder, M. Korzhanova, A. D. Ksenafontov, A. A. Perederiy, O. V. Ohlopkova
Original abstract (English)
Spontaneous mutations often arise and accumulate in the genome of RNA-containing viruses, for example measles, rubella and mumps. Modern requirements for the production of vaccine products containing live vaccine viruses suggest control of the genetic stability of production strains. The method of high throughput sequencing allows to determine all mutations arisen in viral population, and to assess genetic stability of production strains. Analysis of nucleotide sequence of the genomes of seed and production strains of measles (Leningrad-16), rubella (RA27/3) and mumps (Leningrad-3) viruses, in comparison with the reference nucleotide sequences of vaccine strains genomes (submitted in the Gen-Bank database). MATERIALS AND METHODS. Production strains of vaccines (measles culture live, rubella culture live and mumps culture live) were used in this study. Whole genome sequencing was performed using the MGI DNBSEQ G-400 and Illumina NextSeq2000 genetic analyzers. When processing and analyzing the sequencing data, the following programs and tools were used: FastQC v.0.12.1, FastP v.0.23.4, BWA-MEM2 v.2.2.1, VizCoV tool. Analysis of single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) within the viral population in the studied samples revealed a number of minor variants that, and after further accumulation, may lead to changes in the consensus sequences of the vaccine strains proteins. For the studied measles virus samples, no SNPs were found in the main measles virus antigen H protein, however, a nonsynonymous polymorphism was detected in the F protein, occurring in 30.0–40.9% of the viral genomes. SNPs were found in the capsid protein C and in the surface glycoprotein E2 rubella virus, occurring in the viral population with a frequency of up to 30%. SNPs were found in surface antigens hemagglutinin neuraminidase HN and fusion protein F of mumps virus, occurring in the genomes of less than 20% of viral population. Mutations in the genomes of production strains that could affect the immunogenicity of measles, rubella, and mumps vaccines were not detected. The identified set of minor variants with a frequency of occurrence in viral population of more than 10%, which, with further accumulation, can lead to changes in the consensus sequences of vaccine strains, makes the monitoring of vaccine strains stability using high-throughput sequencing relevant.

急诊科国家样本中对 麻疹、腮腺炎、风疹 疫苗 的知识、接受和接受方面的差距

Gaps in Knowledge, Receipt, and Acceptance of Measles, Mumps, Rubella Vaccines in a National Sample of Emergency Department
作者： Sahithi Malireddy, Alexandra Eftimie, Shaokui Ge, Christopher Alvarez, Jesus Ricardo Torres, Brian Chinnock, Michael Gottlieb, Vijaya Arun Kumar, Kristin Rising, Efrat Rosenzweig Kean
摘要中文翻译美国的 麻疹 病例不断增加,引起了人们对成人 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 麻疹 之间差距的担忧,而当前的 麻疹 很难捕捉到这一点。急诊科 (ED) 充当服务不足人群的安全网,可以帮助确定成人 MMR 吸收率的差距。我们评估了全国范围内成人 MMR 的最新状态、知识以及在 ED 接受 疫苗接种 的意愿。我们于 2024 年 4 月至 12 月在美国 10 个急诊室对 18-64 岁的成年人进行了一项横断面调查。参与者完成了一项结构化调查,评估 MMR 知识、自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态、未收到的原因以及在急诊室接受 MMR 的意愿。结果总结为具有 95% 置信区间 (CI) 的比例。多变量逻辑回归确定了与不及时更新和愿意接受 疫苗接种 相关的因素。在 2,456 名参与者中,25.0% (95% CI 23.3–26.7%) 没有听说过 MMR 疫苗,44.0% (42.5–46.4%) 没有了解最新情况。与未接种疫苗相关的因素包括男性(OR 2.21 [1.84–2.66])、非裔美国人(非西班牙裔)种族(OR 2.27 [1.75–2.96])、西班牙裔(OR 1.98 [1.49–2.62])、非英语国家(OR 1.53 [1.12–2.09])和缺乏初级保健访问(或 1.25 [1.01–1.58])。在未及时更新的人中,36.5% (33.6–39.4%) 愿意接受 MMR 疫苗(如果在 ED 中提供)。成人 MMR 疫苗接种 的巨大差距仍然存在,特别是在服务不足的人群中。基于 ED 的策略可能会在 传播 增加期间支持有针对性的 疫苗 教育和交付。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
美国的 麻疹 病例不断增加,引起了人们对成人 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 麻疹 之间差距的担忧,而当前的 麻疹 很难捕捉到这一点。
设计与对象
我们于 2024 年 4 月至 12 月对美国 10 个急诊科 (ED) 18 岁至 64 岁的成年人进行了一项横断面调查。
核心方法
参与者完成了一项结构化调查,评估 MMR 知识、自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态、未收到 MMR 的原因以及在急诊室接受 MMR 的意愿。多变量逻辑回归确定了与不及时更新和愿意接受 疫苗接种 相关的因素。
主要结果
在 2,456 名参与者中,25.0% (95% CI 23.3–26.7%) 没有听说过 MMR 疫苗,44.0% (42.5–46.4%) 没有了解最新情况。在未及时更新的人中,36.5% (33.6–39.4%) 愿意接受 MMR 疫苗(如果在 ED 中提供)。
解释与创新
基于 ED 的策略可能会在 传播 增加期间支持有针对性的 疫苗 教育和交付。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改善服务不足人群的 疫苗接种 外展和教育,特别是那些获得初级保健机会有限的人群。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Gaps in Knowledge, Receipt, and Acceptance of Measles, Mumps, Rubella Vaccines in a National Sample of Emergency Department

Authors: Sahithi Malireddy, Alexandra Eftimie, Shaokui Ge, Christopher Alvarez, Jesus Ricardo Torres, Brian Chinnock, Michael Gottlieb, Vijaya Arun Kumar, Kristin Rising, Efrat Rosenzweig Kean
Original abstract (English)
Measles cases are increasing in the U.S., raising concern about gaps in adult measles, mumps, and rubella (MMR) vaccination, which is poorly captured by current surveillance. Emergency departments (EDs) act as safety nets for underserved populations and can help identify gaps in adult MMR uptake. We assessed adult MMR up-to-date status, knowledge, and willingness to receive vaccination in the ED, nationally. We conducted a cross-sectional survey of adults aged 18–64 at ten U.S. EDs from April–December 2024. Participants completed a structured survey assessing MMR knowledge, self-reported vaccination status, reasons for non-receipt, and willingness to receive MMR in the ED. Outcomes were summarized as proportions with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Multivariable logistic regression identified factors associated with not being up to date and willingness to accept vaccination. Among 2,456 participants, 25.0% (95% CI 23.3–26.7%) had not heard of the MMR vaccine, and 44.0% (42.5–46.4%) were not up to date. Factors associated with being unvaccinated included male sex (OR 2.21 [1.84–2.66]), African American (non-Hispanic) race (OR 2.27 [1.75–2.96]), Hispanic ethnicity (OR 1.98 [1.49–2.62]), non-English speakers (OR 1.53 [1.12–2.09]), and lack of primary care access (OR 1.25 [1.01–1.58]). Among those not up to date, 36.5% (33.6–39.4%) were willing to receive MMR vaccine if offered in the ED. Substantial gaps in adult MMR vaccination persist, especially among underserved populations. ED-based strategies might support targeted vaccine education and delivery during periods of increased transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles cases are increasing in the U.S., raising concern about gaps in adult measles, mumps, and rubella (MMR) vaccination, which is poorly captured by current surveillance.
Design and population
We conducted a cross-sectional survey of adults aged 18–64 at ten U.S. Emergency departments (EDs) from April–December 2024.
Core methods
Participants completed a structured survey assessing MMR knowledge, self-reported vaccination status, reasons for non-receipt, and willingness to receive MMR in the ED. Multivariable logistic regression identified factors associated with not being up to date and willingness to accept vaccination.
Main results
Among 2,456 participants, 25.0% (95% CI 23.3–26.7%) had not heard of the MMR vaccine, and 44.0% (42.5–46.4%) were not up to date. Among those not up to date, 36.5% (33.6–39.4%) were willing to receive MMR vaccine if offered in the ED.
Interpretation and novelty
ED-based strategies might support targeted vaccine education and delivery during periods of increased transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for improved vaccination outreach and education in underserved populations, particularly those with limited access to primary care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

俄罗斯联邦活跃的 麻疹 监测:实施原则和当前挑战

Active measles surveillance in the Russian Federation: implementation principles and current challenges
作者： Olga V. Tsvirkun, N. V. Turaeva, T. N. Moskaleva, L. B. Kisteneva, Natalia A. Antipyat, Т Н Москалева, Л. Б. Кистенева, Т. Н. Москалева
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估主动麻疹 监测在识别真实麻疹病例方面的有效性,并评估院前诊断的质量。背景强调需要改进 监测 方法来解决漏报和诊断不准确的问题。
设计与对象
对2017年至2025年的数据进行回顾性分析,包括通过主动监测识别的患者流行病学调查表1.691份和住院患者的医院记录2.517份。该研究重点关注俄罗斯联邦通过活跃的 麻疹 发现的 麻疹 病例。
核心方法
使用免疫酶测定(IgM/IgG)进行实验室验证。应用描述性流行病学和统计方法来分析数据。
主要结果
Active surveillance annually detected missed measles cases ranging from 1 to 743 in different years. At the pre-hospital stage, measles was not recognized in 37.7 % of hospitalized patients. Over 90 % of cases in recent years exhibited a typical clinical picture, and complications were registered in 38–40 % of patients.
解释与创新
漏诊中典型 麻疹 病例的比例很高,表明临床 麻疹 存在系统性缺陷。该研究深入了解了 麻疹 监测 和 诊断 在俄罗斯联邦面临的挑战。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了持续培训医务人员、加强实验室监测和维持高 麻疹 覆盖率以维持 麻疹 消除状态的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,这可能会引入偏差并限制研究结果的普遍性。由于依赖历史记录且缺乏前瞻性数据收集,证据强度中等。

Active measles surveillance in the Russian Federation: implementation principles and current challenges

Authors: Olga V. Tsvirkun, N. V. Turaeva, T. N. Moskaleva, L. B. Kisteneva, Natalia A. Antipyat, Т Н Москалева, Л. Б. Кистенева, Т. Н. Москалева
Original abstract (English)
From 2004 to 2006, a pilot project was carried out in 11 territories of the Russian Federation to test the method of active epidemiological surveillance, which makes it possible to identify the true number of measles patients among persons with maculopapular rash and fever, regardless of the primary diagnosis. To provide a clinical and epidemiological characterization of measles cases detected through active surveillance and to assess the quality of pre-hospital diagnosis. Materials and Methods. A retrospective analysis of data from 2017–2025 was conducted, including 1.691 epidemiological investigation forms for patients identified through active surveillance and 2.517 hospital records of inpatients. Laboratory verification was performed using immunoenzyme assay (IgM/IgG). Descriptive epidemiological and statistical methods were applied. Active surveillance annually detected missed measles cases (ranging from 1 to 743 in different years). The average age of the patients was 22.5 years. At the pre-hospital stage, measles was not recognized in 37.7 % of hospitalized patients. Over 90 % of cases in recent years exhibited a typical clinical picture, indicating low clinical awareness among physicians. Complications were registered in 38–40 % of patients, with infants under one year of age remaining the most vulnerable group. The high proportion of typical measles cases among those missed indicates systemic deficiencies in clinical diagnosis. Continuous training of medical personnel, enhanced laboratory monitoring, and maintenance of high vaccination coverage are necessary maintain measles elimination status.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of active measles surveillance in identifying true measles cases and to assess the quality of pre-hospital diagnosis. The background highlights the need for improved surveillance methods to address underreporting and diagnostic inaccuracies.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of data from 2017–2025 was conducted, including 1.691 epidemiological investigation forms for patients identified through active surveillance and 2.517 hospital records of inpatients. The study focused on measles cases detected through active surveillance in the Russian Federation.
Core methods
Laboratory verification was performed using immunoenzyme assay (IgM/IgG). Descriptive epidemiological and statistical methods were applied to analyze the data.
Main results
Active surveillance annually detected missed measles cases ranging from 1 to 743 in different years. At the pre-hospital stage, measles was not recognized in 37.7 % of hospitalized patients. Over 90 % of cases in recent years exhibited a typical clinical picture, and complications were registered in 38–40 % of patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The high proportion of typical measles cases among those missed indicates systemic deficiencies in clinical diagnosis. The study provides insights into the challenges of measles surveillance and diagnosis in the Russian Federation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of continuous training of medical personnel, enhanced laboratory monitoring, and maintaining high vaccination coverage to sustain measles elimination status.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data, which may introduce bias and limit the generalizability of findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on historical records and the absence of prospective data collection.

开放子社区中的 麻疹 传播 和 监测

Measles transmission and surveillance in open subcommunities
作者： Niket Thakkar
摘要中文翻译我们经常遇到这样的情况:我们的 麻疹 传播 模型陷入了粗略的人口规模。本文旨在提高其分辨率,或者换句话说,为人口的子社区创建一致的模型。我们特别注意这样一个事实:即使全部人口被隔离,子社区也对 麻疹 输入和人口流动的相关后果开放。事实证明,解决问题可以有效地构建为约束优化问题,其中约束是对粗略模型的聚合。这种数学框架使我们能够在大人口规模上进行流行病学驱动的近似,在这种情况下它们更有效,这使得精细子社区规模的估计变得非典型的非结构化。我们在 疫苗接种 时代之前的英国 60 个城市数据集的背景下开发了这个想法,为所有 60 个城市构建了 传播 模型。我们发现它们具有直观的季节性、波动性和进口元素,但它们依赖于病例报告概率的重要动态。我们详细讨论了这些 监测 过程的潜在解释,并且我们还描述了它们如何影响对竞争性神秘 传播 和瞬态消除动力学之间的平衡的推断。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本文解决了在子社区层面对 麻疹 传播 更详细模型的需求,因为现有模型仅限于粗略的人口规模。该研究旨在提高这些模型的分辨率,以更好地捕捉 麻疹 在特定子社区内传播的复杂性。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 疫苗接种 时代之前的 60 个英国城市数据集,检查所有 60 个城市的 麻疹 传播,以了解子社区如何与 麻疹 输入和人口流动相互作用。该分析是在这个历史数据集中进行的,以探索疾病传播的动态。
核心方法
该研究采用数学建模将解决问题框架为约束优化问题,其中约束是粗模型的聚合。这种方法允许在大规模人口规模上开发流行病学驱动的近似值,同时保持对亚社区特定动态的关注。
主要结果
这些模型表明,子社区中的 麻疹 传播 表现出直观的季节性、波动性和进口元素,但它们也依赖于病例报告概率中的重要动态。这些发现凸显了在更精细的尺度上建模 麻疹 的复杂性。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为证明了在 麻疹 建模中考虑子社区特定动态的重要性,这可以为 传播 模式提供更准确的见解。这种方法的新颖之处在于它对历史数据的应用以及对子社区解决方案的关注。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为改进 麻疹 和子社区中 麻疹 的建模策略提供信息,这可能会加强公共卫生干预和风险评估。它们还深入探讨了病例报告在形成流行病学理解方面的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 疫苗接种 时代之前的历史数据,可能无法完全代表当前的 传播 动态。证据是中等的,因为分析基于单个数据集并依赖于数学建模假设。

Measles transmission and surveillance in open subcommunities

Authors: Niket Thakkar
Original abstract (English)
We're often in a situation where we have models of measles transmission that are stuck at a coarse population scale. This paper is about increasing their resolution or, said differently, creating consistent models for the population's subcommunities. We pay special attention to the fact that even if the full population is isolated, subcommunities are open to measles importations and related consequences of population movement. It turns out that the resolution issue can be productively framed as a constrained optimization problem, where the constraint is aggregation to the coarse model. That mathematical scaffolding allows us to make epidemiologically motivated approximations concentrated at the large population scale, where they're more valid, which leaves the estimates at the fine subcommunity scale atypically unstructured. We develop the idea in the context of the pre-vaccination-era 60 city UK dataset, building transmission models for all 60 cities. We find that they have intuitive seasonality, volatility, and importation elements, but that they depend on nontrivial dynamics in their case reporting probabilities. Potential interpretations of those surveillance processes are discussed in some detail, and we also describe how they influence inference on the balance between competing cryptic transmission and transient elimination dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This paper addresses the need for more detailed models of measles transmission at the subcommunity level, as existing models are limited to a coarse population scale. The study aims to improve the resolution of these models to better capture the complexities of measles spread within specific subcommunities.
Design and population
The study focuses on the pre-vaccination-era 60 city UK dataset, examining measles transmission across all 60 cities to understand how subcommunities interact with measles importations and population movement. The analysis is conducted within this historical dataset to explore the dynamics of disease spread.
Core methods
The research employs mathematical modeling to frame the resolution issue as a constrained optimization problem, where the constraint is the aggregation to the coarse model. This approach allows for the development of epidemiologically motivated approximations at the large population scale, while maintaining the focus on subcommunity-specific dynamics.
Main results
The models reveal that measles transmission in subcommunities exhibits intuitive seasonality, volatility, and importation elements, but they also depend on nontrivial dynamics in their case reporting probabilities. These findings highlight the complexity of modeling measles at a finer scale.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these results as demonstrating the importance of considering subcommunity-specific dynamics in measles modeling, which can provide more accurate insights into transmission patterns. This approach is novel in its application to historical data and its focus on subcommunity resolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform improved surveillance and modeling strategies for measles in subcommunities, which may enhance public health interventions and risk assessments. They also provide insights into the role of case reporting in shaping epidemiological understanding.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to historical data from the pre-vaccination era, which may not fully represent current transmission dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as the analysis is based on a single dataset and relies on mathematical modeling assumptions.

Sốt phát ban nghi sởi tại Nghệ An năm 2024–2025:Phân bố theo nhóm tuổi nguy cơ và địa bàn xã mới từ số liệu giám sát 可疑2024-2025 年义安省 麻疹 病例:基于 麻疹 数据的年龄组和新公社的描述性分析

Sốt phát ban nghi sởi tại Nghệ An năm 2024–2025: Phân bố theo nhóm tuổi nguy cơ và địa bàn xã mới từ số liệu giám sát Suspected Measles Cases in Nghe An during 2024–2025: A descriptive analysis of age group and new communes based on surveillance data
作者： Lợi Nguyễn Thành, Trang Chu Trọng, Di Nguyễn Trọng, Thảo Đậu Thị Bích, Bích Nguyễn Thị Ngọc, Thư Nguyễn Vũ Minh, Dương Trương Hoàng, Liên Trần Hoàng Mỹ, Tuấn Lê Anh, Loi Nguyen Thanh
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2024–2025年,越南 Nghệ An 地区报告的疑似麻疹病例数量显著高于2019–2023年的平均水平。本研究旨在描述该疾病的流行病学特征,重点关注时间分布、年龄组和新乡镇的分布情况。
设计与对象
Nghiên cứu loạt ca bệnh hồi cứu được thực hiện tại Nghệ An từ 01/2024 đến 12/2025, tập trung vào các ca bệnh sốt phát ban nghi sởi được ghi nhận qua hệ thống giám sát. Tổng cộng có 4.531 ca được ghi nhận, bao gồm cả trẻ em dưới 5 tuổi và người lớn hơn.
核心方法
结果数据来源于监测系统,描述了按年龄组和乡镇的分布情况。病例根据年龄和地理区域进行分类,并确定了随时间变化的发病率。
主要结果
Trong tổng số 4.531 ca SPB nghi sởi,trẻ dưới 5 tuổi chiếm tỷ trọng cao nhất (27,8%),trong khi nhóm ≥ 5 tuổi chiếm 40,5%。2024 年 3 月 2025 年(1.296 小时),请注意。
解释与创新
Sự gia tăng số ca mắc ở các nhóm tuổi lớn hơn cho thấy sự tồn tại của khoảng trống miễn dịch trong cộng đồng,请注意,请注意以下事项。Các xã có tỷ lệ mắc cao nhất chủ yếu ở vùng cao và biên giới。
科研与公卫意义
Kết quả cho thấy cần ưu tiên nguồn lực dự phòng cho các xã có tỷ lệ mắc cao,đặc biệt là ở vùng cao và biên giới,请注意,这可能是您的错误。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于监测数据,缺乏关于检测方法或统计分析的详细信息。然而,所提供的数据可以支持对流行病学特征的准确评估。

Sốt phát ban nghi sởi tại Nghệ An năm 2024–2025: Phân bố theo nhóm tuổi nguy cơ và địa bàn xã mới từ số liệu giám sát Suspected Measles Cases in Nghe An during 2024–2025: A descriptive analysis of age group and new communes based on surveillance data

Authors: Lợi Nguyễn Thành, Trang Chu Trọng, Di Nguyễn Trọng, Thảo Đậu Thị Bích, Bích Nguyễn Thị Ngọc, Thư Nguyễn Vũ Minh, Dương Trương Hoàng, Liên Trần Hoàng Mỹ, Tuấn Lê Anh, Loi Nguyen Thanh
Original abstract (English)
Năm 2024-2025, Nghệ An ghi nhận số trường hợp SPB nghi sởi cao vượt mức so với trung bình giai đoạn 2019-2023. Nghiên cứu loạt ca bệnh hồi cứu nhằm mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ, tập trung vào sự phân bố theo thời gian, nhóm tuổi và địa bàn xã mới thông qua hệ thống giám sát từ 01/2024 - 12/2025. Kết quả ghi nhận 4.531 ca SPB nghi sởi, (từ 0-81 tuổi), tập trung ở trẻ<5 tuổi (nhóm từ 2- <5 tuổi chiếm tỷ trọng cao nhất 27,8%). Nhóm ≥ 5 tuổi (chiếm 40,5%), cho thấy bệnh đã xuất hiện nhiều ở các nhóm tuổi lớn hơn, phản ánh sự tồn tại của khoảng trống miễn dịch trong cộng đồng. 588/712 ca sởi xét nghiệm dương tính (82,6%) cho thấy 100% có sốt, phát ban, 99,5% biến chứng viêm phổi và 86,7% chưa tiêm vắc xin sởi. Các xã có tỷ lệ mắc cao nhất chủ yếu ở vùng cao, biên giới. Theo thời gian, số mắc tăng dần vào nửa cuối năm 2024 và đạt đỉnh vào tháng 3/2025 (1.296 trường hợp), sau đó giảm dần. Cần ưu tiên nguồn lực dự phòng cho các xã khó khăn, đẩy mạnh truyền thông nguy cơ phù hợp với đặc điểm văn hoá, ngôn ngữ của đồng bào dân tộc thiểu số về lợi ích của vắc-xin, vận động người dân tham gia tiêm chủng, đồng thời duy trì hệ thống dữ liệu giám sát chặt chẽ.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Năm 2024–2025, Nghệ An ghi nhận số trường hợp sốt phát ban nghi sởi cao vượt mức so với trung bình giai đoạn 2019–2023. Nghiên cứu này nhằm mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ của bệnh, tập trung vào sự phân bố theo thời gian, nhóm tuổi và địa bàn xã mới.
Design and population
Nghiên cứu loạt ca bệnh hồi cứu được thực hiện tại Nghệ An từ 01/2024 đến 12/2025, tập trung vào các ca bệnh sốt phát ban nghi sởi được ghi nhận qua hệ thống giám sát. Tổng cộng có 4.531 ca được ghi nhận, bao gồm cả trẻ em dưới 5 tuổi và người lớn hơn.
Core methods
Kết quả được thu thập từ hệ thống giám sát, mô tả phân bố theo nhóm tuổi và địa bàn xã. Các ca bệnh được phân loại dựa trên lứa tuổi và khu vực địa lý, đồng thời xác định tỷ lệ mắc bệnh theo thời gian.
Main results
Trong tổng số 4.531 ca SPB nghi sởi, trẻ dưới 5 tuổi chiếm tỷ trọng cao nhất (27,8%), trong khi nhóm ≥ 5 tuổi chiếm 40,5%. Số ca mắc tăng dần vào nửa cuối năm 2024 và đạt đỉnh vào tháng 3/2025 (1.296 trường hợp), sau đó giảm dần.
Interpretation and novelty
Sự gia tăng số ca mắc ở các nhóm tuổi lớn hơn cho thấy sự tồn tại của khoảng trống miễn dịch trong cộng đồng, điều này có thể phản ánh sự suy giảm tỷ lệ tiêm chủng. Các xã có tỷ lệ mắc cao nhất chủ yếu ở vùng cao và biên giới.
Scientific and public-health significance
Kết quả cho thấy cần ưu tiên nguồn lực dự phòng cho các xã có tỷ lệ mắc cao, đặc biệt là ở vùng cao và biên giới, đồng thời đẩy mạnh truyền thông về lợi ích của vắc-xin sởi.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nghiên cứu dựa trên dữ liệu giám sát, không có thông tin chi tiết về các phương pháp xét nghiệm hoặc phân tích thống kê. Tuy nhiên, dữ liệu được cung cấp có thể hỗ trợ đánh giá dịch tễ học một cách chính xác.
