大流行期间波兰孕妇对 COVID-19 疫苗 的遵守情况

COVID-19 vaccine compliance among pregnant women in Poland during the pandemic
作者： Hinca K, Jassem-Bobowicz J, Jańczewska I, Drążkowska I, Domżalska-Popadiuk I, Wierzba J
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估大流行期间波兰孕妇对 疫苗 的依从性,解决了解影响该人群 疫苗 决策的因素的需要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了波兰孕妇 队列研究 的数据,重点关注 疫苗接种 的状态、时间和人口特征,例如年龄、教育程度和居住地。
核心方法
研究人员进行了多变量逻辑回归分析,以评估居住地、年龄和教育程度对 疫苗接种 决策的联合影响,并使用调整后的比值比 (aOR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI) 来量化关联。
主要结果
More than 50% of respondents received the COVID-19 vaccine during pregnancy. Vaccination timing was most common in the second trimester (41.6%) and third trimester (31.9%). Lower educational attainment was associated with significantly lower odds of vaccination, with primary education showing an OR of 0.192 (95% CI 0.066–0.561, p = 0.003) and secondary education showing an OR of 0.266 (95% CI 0.182–0.387, p < 0.001) compared to university education. Residence in a large city was associated with higher odds of vaccination during pregnancy (OR = 1.581, 95% CI 1.050–2.378, p = 0.028).
解释与创新
研究结果表明,受教育程度显着影响孕妇的 疫苗接种 决定,强调在产前护理中需要制定量身定制的沟通策略,以提高 疫苗 的吸收率。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以解决孕妇(特别是教育程度较低的孕妇)疫苗 依从性方面的差异,并改善产前环境中的 疫苗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据是中等的,因为它依赖于自我报告的数据和观察分析,这可能会引入偏差。样本量和人口统计分布也可能限制普遍性。

COVID-19 vaccine compliance among pregnant women in Poland during the pandemic

Authors: Hinca K, Jassem-Bobowicz J, Jańczewska I, Drążkowska I, Domżalska-Popadiuk I, Wierzba J
Original abstract (English)
IntroductionThis study aimed to assess COVID-19 vaccination adherence and identify the factors associated with vaccine uptake among pregnant women in Poland during the SARS-CoV-2 pandemic. Given the high risk of severe COVID-19 complications, including acute respiratory failure, multiple organ failure, and maternal and neonatal mortality, understanding the factors that influence adherence to immunization guidelines is critical for optimizing maternal–fetal public health strategies.MethodsThis cross-sectional study analyzed 675 questionnaires collected from women hospitalized in the Department of Neonatology at the University Clinical Center in Gdańsk between November 2021 and February 2022. Older age and residence in a large city were associated with higher odds of receving the vaccine in selected models, whereas obstetric history was not significantly associated with vaccination status.ConclusionWhile vaccine uptake was moderate, the findings highlight a significant demographic divide. These findings suggest that tailored, evidence-based vaccine communication during antenatal care is particularly relevant for women with lower educational attainment. Materials and methodsThis cross-sectional study was conducted among postpartum women hospitalized between 18 November 2021 and 28 February 2022, at the Department of Neonatology, University Clinical Center (UCC), Gdańsk, Poland.Data were collected using a structured questionnaire covering past SARS-CoV-2 infections, COVID-19 vaccination status, demographic characteristics, and obstetric history (subfertility and prior miscarriage). The analysis evaluated associations between vaccination status and vaccine type and demographic characteristics, place of residence, and obstetric history, including fertility disorders and prio...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate vaccine compliance among pregnant women in Poland during the pandemic, addressing the need to understand factors influencing vaccination decisions in this population.
Design and population
The study analyzed data from a cohort of pregnant women in Poland, focusing on vaccination status, timing, and demographic characteristics such as age, education, and place of residence.
Core methods
The researchers conducted multivariable logistic regression analyses to assess the joint effects of place of residence, age, and education on vaccination decisions, using adjusted odds ratios (aOR) and 95% confidence intervals (CI) to quantify associations.
Main results
More than 50% of respondents received the COVID-19 vaccine during pregnancy. Vaccination timing was most common in the second trimester (41.6%) and third trimester (31.9%). Lower educational attainment was associated with significantly lower odds of vaccination, with primary education showing an OR of 0.192 (95% CI 0.066–0.561, p = 0.003) and secondary education showing an OR of 0.266 (95% CI 0.182–0.387, p < 0.001) compared to university education. Residence in a large city was associated with higher odds of vaccination during pregnancy (OR = 1.581, 95% CI 1.050–2.378, p = 0.028).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that educational attainment significantly influences vaccination decisions among pregnant women, highlighting the need for tailored communication strategies in antenatal care to improve vaccine uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform targeted public health interventions to address disparities in vaccine compliance among pregnant women, particularly those with lower educational attainment, and improve vaccination strategies in antenatal settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is moderate, as it relies on self-reported data and observational analysis, which may introduce bias. The sample size and demographic distribution may also limit generalizability.

罗马尼亚西南部孕妇中存在抗风疹抗体 血清流行率

Anti-Rubella antibodies seroprevalence among pregnant women in South-Western Romania
作者： Radoi-Troaca L, Cristea O, Zlatian O, Balasoiu M, Ghenea A, Boldeanu L, Iliescu D
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估罗马尼亚西南部孕妇的抗风疹抗体 血清流行率,以评估免疫水平并确定 疫苗接种 覆盖率的潜在差距。这一点很重要,因为如果母亲在怀孕期间没有免疫力,胎儿就有患先天性风疹综合症的风险。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究于 2013 年至 2022 年间在罗马尼亚西南部进行,重点关注妊娠早期的孕妇。参与者根据出生年份分为四个亚组,反映根据国家免疫计划的 疫苗接种 资格。
核心方法
使用电化学发光免疫分析 (ECLIA) 在 Cobas e601 分析仪上进行抗风疹 IgM 和 IgG 抗体的血清学检测。该研究使用具有 95% 置信区间 (CI) 的 患病率 比率 (PR) 来比较 疫苗接种 资格 队列研究 和抗体 血清流行率 之间的关联,并对零计数细胞应用连续性校正。
主要结果
The seroprevalence of acute rubella infection (IgM positive) was low and slightly decreased from 0.54% (2013–2016) to 0.50% (2019–2022). In rural areas, the highest seroprevalence was observed in women born between 1995 and 1996 (vaccination gap subgroup), while the lowest was in those born between 1989 and 1994 (eligible for RCV vaccine). The proportion of women tested in urban areas decreased significantly from Group 1 (223/367, 60.49%) to Group 2 (1,169/2,669, 43.80%; p < 0.001).
解释与创新
作者解释说,这些发现表明急性风疹感染率略有下降,特别是在农村地区和年轻女性中,这表明 疫苗接种 覆盖率有可能提高。然而,他们强调,一些比较的宽置信区间表明需要进一步研究来确认这些趋势。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果深入了解了罗马尼亚西南部孕妇当前的免疫状况,这对于制定有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略和预防先天性风疹综合征至关重要。他们还强调了解决特定人口亚群体中 疫苗接种 差距的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用横截面设计,这排除了因果推论,以及由于样本量较小而导致某些比较的置信区间较宽。该证据的强度中等,因为结果基于血清学数据,没有通过额外的测试方法明确确认急性感染。

Anti-Rubella antibodies seroprevalence among pregnant women in South-Western Romania

Authors: Radoi-Troaca L, Cristea O, Zlatian O, Balasoiu M, Ghenea A, Boldeanu L, Iliescu D
Original abstract (English)
The objective of the study was to assess the seroprevalence of anti-rubella IgM and IgG antibodies among pregnant women in South-Western Romania, with a focus on temporal trends, demographic factors, and the impact of vaccination eligibility.MethodsThis observational, cross-sectional study included pregnant women screened for TORCH infections at the County Clinical Emergency Hospital of Craiova, Romania, during two periods: 2013–2016 and 2019–2022. Enhanced education and access to vaccination are essential to achieve the WHO eradication target and prevent CRS.Keywords: antibodies, congenital infections, pregnant women, rubella, seroprevalence studies 2.Material and methods2.1.Study design and participantsThis observational, cross-sectional study included pregnant women from South-Western Romania who presented for routine TORCH screening at the Gynecology Department of the County Clinical Emergency Hospital of Craiova, Romania. Two study periods were analyzed (2013–2016 and 2019–2022). AbstractIntroductionRubella infection during pregnancy poses significant risks, including congenital rubella syndrome (CRS), miscarriage, and fetal death. Vaccination programs have reduced rubella incidence globally, but regional differences in coverage and immunity persist. Serum s...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the seroprevalence of anti-rubella antibodies among pregnant women in South-Western Romania to evaluate immunity levels and identify potential gaps in vaccination coverage. This is important due to the risk of congenital rubella syndrome in fetuses if mothers are not immune during pregnancy.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was conducted in South-Western Romania between 2013 and 2022, focusing on pregnant women during their first trimester. Participants were stratified into four subgroups based on birth year, reflecting vaccination eligibility according to the national immunization schedule.
Core methods
Serological testing for anti-rubella IgM and IgG antibodies was performed using an electrochemiluminescence immunoassay (ECLIA) on the Cobas e601 analyzer. The study used prevalence ratios (PR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI) to compare associations between vaccination eligibility cohorts and antibody seroprevalence, applying a continuity correction for zero-count cells.
Main results
The seroprevalence of acute rubella infection (IgM positive) was low and slightly decreased from 0.54% (2013–2016) to 0.50% (2019–2022). In rural areas, the highest seroprevalence was observed in women born between 1995 and 1996 (vaccination gap subgroup), while the lowest was in those born between 1989 and 1994 (eligible for RCV vaccine). The proportion of women tested in urban areas decreased significantly from Group 1 (223/367, 60.49%) to Group 2 (1,169/2,669, 43.80%; p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a slight decline in acute rubella infection rates, particularly in rural areas and among younger women, suggesting potential improvements in vaccination coverage. However, they emphasized that the wide confidence intervals for some comparisons indicate the need for further research to confirm these trends.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the current immunity status of pregnant women in South-Western Romania, which is crucial for informing targeted vaccination strategies and preventing congenital rubella syndrome. They also highlight the importance of addressing vaccination gaps in specific demographic subgroups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a cross-sectional design, which precludes causal inferences, and the wide confidence intervals for some comparisons due to small sample sizes. The evidence is moderate in strength, as the results are based on serological data without definitive confirmation of acute infection through additional testing methods.

巴西东南部城市癌症患者中 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫力的血清学证据的区域分布

Regional distribution of serological evidence of vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients in a city in southeastern Brazil
作者： de Paula V, Pinto M, Ferreira G, Battaglia D, de Oliveira R, Manchiero C, Dantas B, Tengan F, Abdala E, Magri M
摘要中文翻译本研究评估了居住在巴西圣保罗市的癌症患者中与 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫相一致的血清学证据的区域分布。我们对 2023 年在圣保罗州癌症研究所 (ICESP) 接受乙型肝炎表面抗体 (抗 HBs) 血清学检测的成年患者进行了一项回顾性观察研究。这些发现表明,在圣保罗各地的癌症患者中,特别是老年人和男性中,与 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫相一致的血清学证据频率较低。材料和方法研究设计这项回顾性观察研究包括 2023 年 1 月至 12 月期间在圣保罗大学医学院临床医院 (HCFMUSP) 附属的圣保罗癌症研究所 (ICESP) 接受护理的成年癌症患者。测试是在研究开始前作为常规临床护理的一部分进行的,随后从医院的病历中检索数据。伦理学本研究获得了HCFMUSP 研究项目分析伦理委员会,协议号 6.120.828 和 6.718.762(CAAE:70101521.9.0000.0068)。摘要疫苗接种是预防乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的主要策略,但其在癌症患者中的发生率仍不充分。与 疫苗 诱导的免疫相容的血清学证据被定义为反应性抗 HBs 和非反应性乙型肝炎核心抗体(抗 HBc)。进行单变量和多变量泊松回归分析以确定相关因素。在 1,143 名患者中,平均年龄为 57.4 ± 16.42 岁,61.9% 为女性,60.6% 自认为是白人,76.8% 患有实体瘤。总体...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查巴西东南部一座城市的癌症患者中 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫力的血清学证据的区域分布,以解决该特定人群免疫模式的知识差距。
设计与对象
对 2023 年在圣保罗州癌症研究所 (ICESP) 接受乙型肝炎表面抗体 (抗 HBs) 检测的 1,143 名成年癌症患者进行了一项回顾性观察研究。患者分布在圣保罗市的所有五个区,其中南部和东部所占比例最大。
核心方法
进行单变量和多变量泊松回归分析以确定相关因素。单变量分析中 p 值 < 0.20 的变量被选择用于多变量模型。使用皮尔逊卡方检验评估人口统计特征和癌症类型。
主要结果
Overall, 26.2% (299/1,143) of cancer patients showed serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B. In the univariable analysis, patients in the North had lower prevalence compared to those in the South (20.7% versus 28.9%; PR = 0.72, 95% CI: 0.51-1.01, p = 0.054), but this association was no longer significant after adjustment (p = 0.139). Female patients were more likely than male patients to present serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity (aPR = 1.42, 95% CI: 1.10-1.82).
解释与创新
作者解释说,血清学证据的区域差异可能部分反映了人口分布,因为南方是人口最多的地区。他们指出,观察到与年龄相关的差异,年轻人比老年人表现出更高的免疫力频率。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要在特定地区开展有针对性的 疫苗接种 工作,并强调年龄和性别在免疫模式中的重要性,这可以指导提高癌症患者中乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率的公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为调整后居住区域和免疫力之间的关联在统计上并不显着,并且没有观察到不同区域之间的人口差异。

Regional distribution of serological evidence of vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients in a city in southeastern Brazil

Authors: de Paula V, Pinto M, Ferreira G, Battaglia D, de Oliveira R, Manchiero C, Dantas B, Tengan F, Abdala E, Magri M
Original abstract (English)
This study evaluated the regional distribution of serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients residing in Sao Paulo city, Brazil. We conducted a retrospective observational study of adult patients who underwent hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) serological testing at the Instituto do Cancer do Estado de Sao Paulo (ICESP) in 2023. These findings indicate a low frequency of serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients across Sao Paulo, particularly among older adults and men. MATERIALS AND METHODSStudy designThis retrospective observational study included adult cancer patients receiving care at the Instituto do Cancer do Estado de Sao Paulo (ICESP), affiliated with the Clinical Hospital of the Faculty of Medicine, University of Sao Paulo (HCFMUSP), between January and December 2023. Testing was conducted as part of routine clinical care before the study began, with data subsequently retrieved from the hospital’s medical records.EthicsThis study was approved by the Ethics Committee for Analysis of Research Projects of the HCFMUSP under protocols No 6.120.828 and 6.718.762 (CAAE: 70101521.9.0000.0068). ABSTRACTVaccination is the primary strategy for preventing hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, but its frequency remains insufficiently characterized among cancer patients. Serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity was defined as reactive anti-HBs and non-reactive hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc). Univariable and multivariable Poisson regression analyses were performed to identify associated factors. Among 1,143 patients, the mean age was 57.4 ± 16.42 years, 61.9% were female, 60.6% self-identified White, and 76.8% had solid tumors. Overal...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the regional distribution of serological evidence of vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients in a city in southeastern Brazil, addressing the knowledge gap regarding immunity patterns in this specific population.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted among 1,143 adult cancer patients who underwent hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) testing at the Instituto do Cancer do Estado de Sao Paulo (ICESP) in 2023. Patients were distributed across all five zones of Sao Paulo city, with the largest proportions in the South and East.
Core methods
Univariable and multivariable Poisson regression analyses were performed to identify associated factors. Variables with p-values < 0.20 in univariable analyses were selected for the multivariable model. Demographic characteristics and cancer type were assessed using Pearson’s chi-square test.
Main results
Overall, 26.2% (299/1,143) of cancer patients showed serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B. In the univariable analysis, patients in the North had lower prevalence compared to those in the South (20.7% versus 28.9%; PR = 0.72, 95% CI: 0.51-1.01, p = 0.054), but this association was no longer significant after adjustment (p = 0.139). Female patients were more likely than male patients to present serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity (aPR = 1.42, 95% CI: 1.10-1.82).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the regional variation in serological evidence may partly reflect demographic distribution, as the South is the most populous region. They noted that age-related differences were observed, with younger adults showing higher frequency of immunity than older individuals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted vaccination efforts in specific regions and highlight the importance of age and sex in immunity patterns, which can guide public health strategies for improving hepatitis B vaccination coverage among cancer patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential confounding factors. The evidence is moderate, as the association between zone of residence and immunity was not statistically significant after adjustment, and demographic differences across zones were not observed.

大型集会活动的时间表驱动的进口风险框架:2026 年 FIFA 世界杯

A schedule-driven importation risk framework for mass gathering events: FIFA World Cup 2026
作者： Herrera-Diestra, J. L., Jamieson, N., Harris, M., Bi, K., Ndeffo-Mbah, M. L., Ertem, Z., Al-amery, A., Meyers, L. A.
摘要中文翻译目标:大型国际活动吸引了世界各地的游客,但除了日常旅行之外,它们给传染病输入风险增加了多少?我们开发了一个日程驱动的、不确定性感知的框架来估计这种额外的风险,并将其应用于 2026 年 FIFA 世界杯作为案例研究。方法:三个嵌套泊松模型代表基线旅行、与事件相关的增长以及按地点和日期划分的额外游客的日程驱动分配,并通过蒙特卡罗模拟传播疾病漏报和感染时旅行概率的不确定性。我们在 2026 年 6 月世界杯小组赛阶段将该方法应用于美国 11 个 宿主 城市的五种疾病,仅使用公开数据,根据独立报告的登革热病例数进行评估,并使用 麻疹 作为额外的定性检查。结果:相对于 2026 年没有比赛的旅行,世界杯在赛程驱动的路由下增加了 1.0-6.5% 的预期输入,而在忽略比赛赛程的路由下增加了 2.9-8.5%。登革热、疟疾和百日咳的模拟输入水平最高,11 个城市中有 10 个城市中至少有 1 例感染登革热或百日咳传入的概率超过 0.99。与 2024 年登革热和 2022 年 麻疹 监测 数据的比较显示,建模和报告的登革热病例之间存在显着的等级相关性 (Spearman rho= 0.83)。结论:这种方法提供了一种基于经验的方法,仅使用免费提供的数据来评估大型国际活动期间的输入风险,从而确定公共卫生规划在哪些方面可能最有用。对于世界杯案例研究来说,一旦球迷按照实际比赛日程安排而不是作为普通旅行者,赛事本身的贡献就微乎其微了。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
大型国际活动吸引了世界各地的游客,但除了日常旅行之外,它们给传染病输入风险增加了多少?
设计与对象
我们开发了一个日程驱动的、不确定性感知的框架来估计这种额外的风险,并将其应用于 2026 年 FIFA 世界杯作为案例研究。
核心方法
三个嵌套的泊松模型代表基线旅行、与事件相关的增长以及按地点和日期划分的额外游客的日程驱动分配,蒙特卡罗模拟传播了疾病漏报和感染时旅行概率的不确定性。
主要结果
相对于 2026 年没有比赛的旅行,世界杯在赛程驱动的路线下增加了 1.0-6.5% 的预期输入,而在忽略比赛日程的路线下则增加了 2.9-8.5%。
解释与创新
这种方法提供了一种基于经验的方法,仅使用免费提供的数据来评估大型国际活动期间的进口风险,从而确定公共卫生规划在哪些方面可能最有用。
科研与公卫意义
对于世界杯案例研究来说,一旦球迷按照实际比赛日程安排而不是作为普通旅行者,赛事本身的贡献就微乎其微了。
局限与证据强度
与 2024 年登革热和 2022 年 麻疹 监测 数据的比较显示,建模和报告的登革热病例之间存在显着的等级相关性 (Spearman rho= 0.83)。

A schedule-driven importation risk framework for mass gathering events: FIFA World Cup 2026

Authors: Herrera-Diestra, J. L., Jamieson, N., Harris, M., Bi, K., Ndeffo-Mbah, M. L., Ertem, Z., Al-amery, A., Meyers, L. A.
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Large international events draw visitors worldwide, but how much do they add to infectious disease importation risk beyond everyday travel? We developed a schedule-driven, uncertainty-aware framework to estimate this added risk and applied it to the 2026 FIFA World Cup as a case study. Methods: Three nested Poisson models represent baseline travel, event-related growth, and schedule-driven allocation of additional visitors by venue and date, with Monte Carlo simulation propagating uncertainty in disease under-reporting and travel-while-infected probability. We applied the approach to five diseases across 11 US host cities during the June 2026 World Cup group stage using only publicly available data, evaluated against independently reported dengue case counts, with measles as an additional qualitative check. Results: Relative to 2026 travel without the tournament, the World Cup added 1.0-6.5% to expected importations under schedule-driven routing, versus 2.9-8.5% under routing that ignores the match schedule. Dengue, malaria, and pertussis had the highest modeled importation levels, and the probability of at least one infected dengue or pertussis arrival exceeded 0.99 at 10 of 11 cities. Comparison with 2024 dengue and 2022 measles surveillance data showed a significant rank correlation between modeled and reported dengue cases (Spearman rho= 0.83). Conclusions: This approach provides an empirically grounded way to assess importation risk during large international events using only freely available data, identifying where public health planning may be most useful. For the World Cup case study, the tournament's own contribution was modest once fans were routed by the actual match schedule rather than as ordinary travelers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Large international events draw visitors worldwide, but how much do they add to infectious disease importation risk beyond everyday travel?
Design and population
We developed a schedule-driven, uncertainty-aware framework to estimate this added risk and applied it to the 2026 FIFA World Cup as a case study.
Core methods
Three nested Poisson models represent baseline travel, event-related growth, and schedule-driven allocation of additional visitors by venue and date, with Monte Carlo simulation propagating uncertainty in disease under-reporting and travel-while-infected probability.
Main results
Relative to 2026 travel without the tournament, the World Cup added 1.0-6.5% to expected importations under schedule-driven routing, versus 2.9-8.5% under routing that ignores the match schedule.
Interpretation and novelty
This approach provides an empirically grounded way to assess importation risk during large international events using only freely available data, identifying where public health planning may be most useful.
Scientific and public-health significance
For the World Cup case study, the tournament's own contribution was modest once fans were routed by the actual match schedule rather than as ordinary travelers.
Limitations and evidence strength
Comparison with 2024 dengue and 2022 measles surveillance data showed a significant rank correlation between modeled and reported dengue cases (Spearman rho= 0.83).

麻疹 预防中免疫球蛋白的使用、可用性和给药注意事项

Immunoglobulin Use, Availability, and Administration Considerations in Measles Prevention
作者： Dustin Le, Amina Ahmed, Michael J. Raschka, Patricia Stinchfield, Jeffery A. Goad
摘要中文翻译摘要 2026年,美国国家传染病基金会(NFID)与美国传染病学会(IDSA)召开了麻疹峰会,以应对美国三十多年来最大规模的麻疹疫情卷土重来,确诊病例超过3000例,其中大部分是未接种疫苗的个体。许多临床医生和公共卫生专业人员的第一手 麻疹 经验有限,这强调了临床指导的必要性。在这篇评论中,我们研究了在供应脆弱和后勤挑战的情况下免疫球蛋白 (IG) 在 麻疹 暴露后预防 (PEP) 中的使用。GamaSTAN® 是唯一获得美国食品和药物管理局 (FDA) 许可的肌内 IG 产品,面临着间歇性供应、不可回收的单瓶经济问题,以及 2013 年建议将 PEP 剂量加倍,从而增加了每位患者的需求。当患者体重超过 30 公斤或以上,超过可以肌内注射足够剂量的免疫球蛋白的体重时,或者当 IMIG 供应受到限制时,IVIG 的使用越来越多,同时批次撤回会损害机构处方。我们回顾了 PEP 的基于证据的患者选择,强调 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 疫苗 在暴露后 72 小时内仍然是一线药物,当 疫苗接种 有禁忌或可能无效时,保留 IG。免疫抑制疗法对活体 疫苗 安全性和有效性的影响差异很大。因此,我们反对将每种生物制剂、免疫调节剂或类固醇归类为自动 MMR 禁忌症,这会高估 IG-PEP 需求并排除符合条件的患者的 MMR。我们提出针对特定类别的分层和电子健康记录决策支持规则,以更好地将 IG 需求与真实需求保持一致。最后,我们解决了由于供体滴度下降而导致的 麻疹 抗体效力标准下降的问题,并提供了 PEP 窗口内的剂量、多部位儿科给药和输注物流方面的指导。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是 麻疹病毒,特别审查了用于 麻疹 暴露后预防 (PEP) 的免疫球蛋白 (IG) 的使用、可用性和给药注意事项。核心问题是如何优化 IG 在 麻疹 预防中的使用,特别是在供应限制和后勤挑战的背景下。
证据基础与方法
该评论是评论,而不是系统评论或荟萃分析。它讨论了 麻疹 在美国重新流行的背景以及围绕 IG 供应的挑战,包括 FDA 许可的 GamaSTAN® 产品的单瓶经济性和 2013 年将 PEP 剂量加倍的建议。它还强调了由于 IMIG 的局限性,IVIG 在某些患者群体中的使用越来越多。
共识与结论
MMR 疫苗 仍然是暴露 72 小时内 麻疹 PEP 的一线干预措施,IG 保留用于 疫苗接种 禁忌或可能无效的情况。由于许多临床医生和公共卫生专业人员的第一手 麻疹 经验有限,它强调了临床指导的必要性。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,免疫抑制疗法对活体 疫苗 安全性和有效性的影响各不相同,并且反对将所有生物制剂、免疫调节剂或类固醇归类为自动 MMR 禁忌症。它还强调了由于供体滴度减弱而导致 麻疹 抗体效力下降,这可能会影响剂量和给药策略。
科研与实践启示
该审查建议对 IG 使用的患者进行特定类别的分层,并建议实施电子健康记录决策支持规则,以更好地将 IG 需求与真实需求结合起来。它还要求在 PEP 窗口内提供剂量、多站点儿科给药和输液物流方面的指导。

Immunoglobulin Use, Availability, and Administration Considerations in Measles Prevention

Authors: Dustin Le, Amina Ahmed, Michael J. Raschka, Patricia Stinchfield, Jeffery A. Goad
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In 2026, the National Foundation for Infectious Diseases (NFID), with the Infectious Diseases Society of America (IDSA), convened a Measles Summit in response to the largest U.S. measles resurgence in over three decades, with more than 3,000 confirmed cases mostly among unvaccinated individuals. Many clinicians and public health professionals have limited firsthand measles experience, underscoring the need for clinical guidance. In this commentary, we examine immune globulin (IG) use for measles post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) amid fragile supply and logistical challenges. GamaSTAN®, the sole Food and Drug Administration (FDA)-licensed intramuscular IG product, faces episodic availability, non-returnable single-vial economics, and a 2013 recommendation to double the PEP dose, increasing per-patient demand. IVIG is increasingly employed when patients weigh 30 kg or more, beyond the weight at which an adequate dose of immune globulin can be given intramuscularly, or when IMIG supply is constrained, while concurrent lot withdrawals compromise institutional formularies. We review evidence-based patient selection for PEP, emphasizing that the Measles, Mumps, and Rubella (MMR) vaccine remains first-line within 72 hours of exposure, with IG reserved when vaccination is contraindicated or potentially ineffective. Immunosuppressive therapies vary widely in their effects on the safety and efficacy of live vaccines. We therefore argue against categorizing every biologic, immunomodulator, or steroid as an automatic MMR contraindication, which would overestimate IG-PEP demand and preclude MMR for eligible patients. We propose class-specific stratification and an electronic health record decision-support rule to better align IG demand with genuine need. Finally, we address declining potency standards for measles antibody, attributable to waning donor titers, and offer guidance on dosing, multi-site pediatric administration, and infusion logistics within the PEP window.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on measles virus, specifically examining the use, availability, and administration considerations of immune globulin (IG) for post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) against measles. The central question is how to optimize IG use in measles prevention, particularly in the context of supply constraints and logistical challenges.
Evidence base and method
The review is a commentary, not a systematic review or meta-analysis. It discusses the context of the measles resurgence in the U.S. and the challenges surrounding IG supply, including the FDA-licensed GamaSTAN® product's single-vial economics and the 2013 recommendation to double the PEP dose. It also highlights the increasing use of IVIG in certain patient populations due to IMIG limitations.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the MMR vaccine remains the first-line intervention for measles PEP within 72 hours of exposure, with IG reserved for cases where vaccination is contraindicated or potentially ineffective. It emphasizes the need for clinical guidance due to limited firsthand measles experience among many clinicians and public health professionals.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that immunosuppressive therapies vary in their effects on the safety and efficacy of live vaccines, and it argues against categorizing all biologics, immunomodulators, or steroids as automatic MMR contraindications. It also highlights the declining potency of measles antibodies due to waning donor titers, which may affect dosing and administration strategies.
Research and practice implications
The review proposes class-specific stratification of patients for IG use and suggests implementing an electronic health record decision-support rule to better align IG demand with genuine need. It also calls for guidance on dosing, multi-site pediatric administration, and infusion logistics within the PEP window.

麻疹病毒 的高效 宿主 传播:从既定结果中解开误解

Efficient host transmission of measles virus: disentangling misconceptions from established findings
作者： Angela Liu, Patrick L. Sinn
摘要中文翻译摘要 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 被认为是最具传染性的人类病毒。这篇评论批判性地研究了 MeV 非凡的传播能力的解释。尽管在理解 MeV 感染、发病机制 和 宿主 传播方面已经取得了实质性进展,但为什么 MeV 比同一分类科中其他结构相似的呼吸道病毒明显更具传播性的基本问题仍未解决。许多常见的解释要么不正确,要么过于简单化,要么没有得到严格的实验证据的充分支持。值得注意的是,直接研究 MeV 传播 的控制机制的研究明显缺乏。这种知识差距之所以持续存在,部分原因是人类是 MeV 的唯一自然 储存宿主,因此最相关的实验模型可能成本高昂、技术上具有挑战性且能力有限。此外,解决 MeV 环境稳定性的研究很大程度上依赖于体外实验室条件,这些条件可能与现实世界的 传播 场景相关性有限。虽然这些研究得出了有用的观察结果,但它们往往缺乏对有效 宿主 到 宿主 传播的明确机制的了解。在这篇综述中,我们讨论了多种策略来研究和定义MeV的有效传播,包括(i)流行病学,(ii)受控环境中的稳定性,(iii)感染剂量,(iv)潜伏期和(v)从呼吸道上皮脱落。正如将要讨论的,MeV 传播 动力学与其他常见呼吸道病毒(例如流感病毒、鼻病毒和呼吸道合胞病毒)的定量、头对头比较明显缺乏。更清楚地了解管理高效 MeV 传播 的机制可能会为减少教育、医疗保健和社区环境中传播的策略提供信息。

范围与问题
麻疹病毒 (MeV) 被认为是最具传染性的人类病毒。这篇评论批判性地研究了 MeV 非凡的传播能力的解释。为什么 MeV 比同一分类科中其他结构相似的呼吸道病毒的传播性明显更高,这一根本问题仍未解决。

科研与实践启示
更清楚地了解管理高效 MeV 传播 的机制可能会为减少教育、医疗保健和社区环境中传播的策略提供信息。正如将要讨论的,MeV 传播 动力学与其他常见呼吸道病毒(例如流感病毒、鼻病毒和呼吸道合胞病毒)的定量、头对头比较明显缺乏。

Efficient host transmission of measles virus: disentangling misconceptions from established findings

Authors: Angela Liu, Patrick L. Sinn
Original abstract (English)
SUMMARY Measles virus (MeV) is considered the most contagious human virus. This review critically examines explanations for MeV’s extraordinary transmissibility. Although substantial progress has been made in understanding MeV infection, pathogenesis, and host spread, the fundamental question of why MeV is markedly more transmissible than other structurally similar respiratory viruses within the same taxonomic families remains unresolved. Many commonly cited explanations are either incorrect, oversimplified, or insufficiently supported by rigorous experimental evidence. Notably, there is a striking lack of studies that directly investigate the mechanisms governing MeV transmission. This knowledge gap persists in part because humans are the only natural reservoir for MeV, and thus the most relevant experimental models can be costly, technically challenging, and limited in ability. Moreover, studies addressing MeV environmental stability have relied largely on in vitro laboratory conditions that may have limited relevance to real-world transmission scenarios. While these studies have yielded useful observations, they often lack definitive mechanistic insight into efficient host-to-host spread. In this review, we discuss multiple strategies to investigate and define the efficient transmission of MeV, including (i) epidemiology, (ii) stability in controlled environments, (iii) infectious dose, (iv) incubation period, and (v) shedding from the respiratory epithelium. As will be discussed, quantitative, head-to-head comparisons of MeV transmission dynamics with those of other common respiratory viruses, such as influenza virus, rhinovirus, and respiratory syncytial virus, are notably absent. A clearer understanding of the mechanisms that govern efficient MeV transmission may inform strategies to reduce spread in educational, healthcare, and community settings.

Studies addressing MeV environmental stability have relied largely on in vitro laboratory conditions that may have limited relevance to real-world transmission scenarios.

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications

2026 年 麻疹 在孟加拉国卷土重来及其公共卫生影响

The 2026 measles resurgence in Bangladesh and its public health implications
作者： Md. Kamrujjaman, Faizunnesa Khondaker, Alvi Ahmed Sarker, Nuzhat Nuari Khan Rivu
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 麻疹 在大流行后时期在全球范围内卷土重来,因为常规免疫恢复仍低于中断 传播 所需的两剂阈值。孟加拉国此前已接近消除 麻疹(风疹),但进入 2026 年时,覆盖范围差距不断扩大,疫苗 库存耗尽,失踪儿童人数不断增加。我们进行了情况分析,以评估 暴发 的规模、集中度和计划影响。方法 我们于 2026 年 4 月 11 日至 15 日使用来自 WHO、UNICEF、DGHS 公告、PubMed/MEDLINE、ReliefWeb、SEARO 更新和孟加拉语媒体的数据进行了快速混合证据审查。在筛选的 46 份记录中,纳入了 19 份。分析基于汇总的、公开的 监测 和程序数据。结果 DGHS 麻疹 日期为 2026 年 4 月 15 日的新闻稿报告了自 3 月 15 日以来的累计总数:19,161 例疑似病例、2,973 例确诊病例、166 例疑似死亡和 32 例确诊死亡;世卫组织 4 月 15 日的最新报告报告了 58 个受影响地区。累计疑似病例负担在空间上集中:达卡和拉杰沙希合计占疑似病例的62.7%,区级HHI为0.255。5 岁以下儿童占病例的 81%,其中 34% 为 9 个月以下婴儿。疫苗接种 状态显示 72% 的零剂量病例和 16% 的部分疫苗接种病例。MR1有效覆盖率从2019年的88.6%下降到2023年的86.1%,而MR2有效覆盖率从89.1%下降到80.7%,表明免疫差距扩大。解释 复苏反映了累积的免疫差距,而不是由地方不平等和计划中断驱动的 疫苗 失败。当务之急包括有针对性的 疫苗接种 活动、恢复维生素 A 补充剂、加强儿科护理能力以及将实时 监测 纳入 暴发 响应中。

范围与问题
由于常规免疫恢复仍低于中断 传播 所需的两剂阈值,麻疹 在大流行后时期在全球范围内卷土重来。孟加拉国此前已接近消除 麻疹(风疹),但进入 2026 年时,覆盖范围差距不断扩大,疫苗 库存耗尽,失踪儿童人数不断增加。
证据基础与方法
我们进行了情况分析,以评估 暴发 的规模、集中度和计划影响。我们于 2026 年 4 月 11 日至 15 日期间使用来自 WHO、UNICEF、DGHS 公告、PubMed/MEDLINE、ReliefWeb、SEARO 更新和孟加拉语媒体的数据进行了快速混合证据审查。
共识与结论
DGHS 麻疹 2026 年 4 月 15 日新闻稿报告了自 3 月 15 日以来累计疑似病例 19,161 例、确诊病例 2,973 例、疑似死亡 166 例和确诊死亡 32 例;世卫组织 4 月 15 日的最新报告报告了 58 个受影响地区。累计疑似病例负担在空间上集中:达卡和拉杰沙希合计占疑似病例的62.7%,区级HHI为0.255。5 岁以下儿童占病例的 81%,其中 34% 为 9 个月以下婴儿。疫苗接种 状态显示 72% 的零剂量病例和 16% 的部分疫苗接种病例。
争议与缺口
MR1有效覆盖率从2019年的88.6%下降到2023年的86.1%,而MR2有效覆盖率从89.1%下降到80.7%,表明免疫差距扩大。这种复苏反映了累积的免疫力差距,而不是由地方不平等和计划中断驱动的 疫苗 失败。
科研与实践启示
当务之急包括有针对性的 疫苗接种 活动、恢复维生素 A 补充剂、加强儿科护理能力以及将实时 监测 纳入 暴发 响应中。

The 2026 measles resurgence in Bangladesh and its public health implications

Authors: Md. Kamrujjaman, Faizunnesa Khondaker, Alvi Ahmed Sarker, Nuzhat Nuari Khan Rivu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Measles has resurged globally in the post-pandemic period as routine immunisation recovery remains below the two-dose threshold required to interrupt transmission. Bangladesh, previously nearing measles–rubella elimination, entered 2026 with widening coverage gaps, depleted vaccine stocks, and increasing numbers of missed children. We conducted a situation analysis to assess the scale, concentration, and programmatic implications of the outbreak. Methods We performed a rapid mixed-evidence review from 11–15 April 2026 using data from WHO, UNICEF, DGHS bulletins, PubMed/MEDLINE, ReliefWeb, SEARO updates, and Bangla-language media. Of 46 records screened, 19 were included. Analysis was based on aggregated, publicly available surveillance and programme data. Findings The DGHS measles press release dated 15 April 2026 reported cumulative totals since 15 March of 19,161 suspected cases, 2,973 confirmed cases, 166 suspected deaths, and 32 confirmed deaths; a WHO update dated 15 April reported 58 affected districts. The cumulative suspected-case burden was spatially concentrated: Dhaka and Rajshahi together accounted for 62.7% of suspected cases, and the division-level HHI was 0.255. Children under five comprised 81% of cases, including 34% infants under nine months. Vaccination status showed 72% zero-dose and 16% partially vaccinated cases. Valid MR1 coverage declined from 88.6% in 2019 to 86.1% in 2023, while valid MR2 coverage declined from 89.1% to 80.7%, indicating a widening immunity gap. Interpretation The resurgence reflects accumulated immunity gaps rather than vaccine failure, driven by subnational inequities and programme disruption. Urgent priorities include targeted vaccination campaigns, restoration of vitamin A supplementation, strengthening paediatric care capacity, and integrating real-time surveillance into outbreak response.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Measles has resurged globally in the post-pandemic period as routine immunisation recovery remains below the two-dose threshold required to interrupt transmission. Bangladesh, previously nearing measles–rubella elimination, entered 2026 with widening coverage gaps, depleted vaccine stocks, and increasing numbers of missed children.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a situation analysis to assess the scale, concentration, and programmatic implications of the outbreak. We performed a rapid mixed-evidence review from 11–15 April 2026 using data from WHO, UNICEF, DGHS bulletins, PubMed/MEDLINE, ReliefWeb, SEARO updates, and Bangla-language media.
Consensus and conclusions
The DGHS measles press release dated 15 April 2026 reported cumulative totals since 15 March of 19,161 suspected cases, 2,973 confirmed cases, 166 suspected deaths, and 32 confirmed deaths; a WHO update dated 15 April reported 58 affected districts. The cumulative suspected-case burden was spatially concentrated: Dhaka and Rajshahi together accounted for 62.7% of suspected cases, and the division-level HHI was 0.255. Children under five comprised 81% of cases, including 34% infants under nine months. Vaccination status showed 72% zero-dose and 16% partially vaccinated cases.
Controversies and gaps
Valid MR1 coverage declined from 88.6% in 2019 to 86.1% in 2023, while valid MR2 coverage declined from 89.1% to 80.7%, indicating a widening immunity gap. The resurgence reflects accumulated immunity gaps rather than vaccine failure, driven by subnational inequities and programme disruption.
Research and practice implications
Urgent priorities include targeted vaccination campaigns, restoration of vitamin A supplementation, strengthening paediatric care capacity, and integrating real-time surveillance into outbreak response.

Long-term survival (≥2 years) in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: a systematic review of prolonged survivors
作者： Ravindra Kumar Garg, Shweta Pandey, Imran Rizvi, Sanjay Singhal
摘要中文翻译目的亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 是一种进行性且往往致命的疾病,生存率差异很大。本系统评价旨在描述长期生存(≥2 年)、缓解和稳定的特征,并确定有利结果的决定因素。方法根据 PRISMA 2020 指南进行系统评价。包括确诊 SSPE 病例的研究被认为符合纳入条件。个体患者分析仅限于随访≥2 年的病例。数据是在患者和 队列研究 级别提取的。结果对 77 名患者的分析表明,超过 2 年的生存期是可以实现的,但存在差异。存活率分布显示 2 至 44.2% 存活
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 的长期生存(≥2 年),这是一种由 麻疹病毒 引起的进行性且常常致命的疾病。核心问题是描述长期幸存者、缓解、稳定的特征,并确定有利结果的决定因素。
证据基础与方法
根据 PRISMA 2020 指南进行了系统审查。该综述旨在分析 SSPE 病例的长期生存、缓解和稳定,资格仅限于确诊的 SSPE 病例。个体患者分析仅限于随访≥2 年的病例。
共识与结论
对 77 名患者的分析表明,超过 2 年的生存期是可以实现的,但存在很大差异。生存分布表明,44.2% 的患者在 2 至 [不完整数据] 之间生存。
争议与缺口
证据受到不完整的生存分布数据的限制,这阻碍了对长期结果的充分了解。没有关于有利结果的决定因素或生存机制的详细讨论。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来完成生存分布分析并探索有助于 SSPE 良好结果的因素。这可以为长期幸存者提供更好的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略。

View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on long-term survival (≥2 years) in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE), a progressive and often fatal disorder caused by the measles virus. The central question is to characterize prolonged survivors, remission, stabilization, and identify determinants of favorable outcomes.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review was conducted according to PRISMA 2020 guidelines. The review aimed to analyze long-term survival, remission, and stabilization in SSPE cases, with eligibility restricted to confirmed SSPE cases. Individual patient analysis was limited to cases with ≥2 years of follow-up.
Consensus and conclusions
Analysis of 77 patients showed that survival beyond 2 years is achievable but highly variable. The survival distribution indicated that 44.2% of patients survived between 2 to [incomplete data].
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited by incomplete survival distribution data, which prevents a full understanding of long-term outcomes. There is no detailed discussion on the determinants of favorable outcomes or the mechanisms underlying survival.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to complete the survival distribution analysis and to explore the factors contributing to favorable outcomes in SSPE. This could inform better surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for prolonged survivors.

针对 RNA 病毒的更广谱直接作用 抗病毒药物

Towards broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals against RNA viruses
作者： Hendrik Jan Thibaut, Dirk Jochmans, Christophe Van Dijck, Manon Laporte, Laurens Liesenborghs, Johan Neyts
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是针对 RNA 病毒的更广谱直接作用 麻疹病毒 (BSA) 的开发,特别是 麻疹病毒。核心问题是 BSA 是否可以开发用于靶向多个 RNA 病毒属、家族甚至跨家族,以及这种方法如何解决治疗选择有限的病毒感染的挑战。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 抗病毒药物 药物开发的系统分析,讨论了 BSA 的基本原理、其生物决定因素和临床开发框架。它不报告具体的数据库、搜索日期或研究计数,但概述了识别和评估 抗病毒药物 抗 RNA 病毒药物的一般方法。
共识与结论
开发 BSA 是解决 抗病毒药物 治疗 针对 RNA 病毒(包括 麻疹病毒)未满足的医疗需求的一种有前景的策略。它强调了靶向保守病毒成分以实现广泛的 抗病毒药物 活性的重要性,并强调了有效评估这些药物的临床框架的必要性。
争议与缺口
审查指出,术语“广谱”抗病毒药物 缺乏明确的定义,建议使用“广谱”代替。它还确定了证据中的差距,例如许多 抗病毒药物 药物的临床前数据有限,以及某些药物缺乏可靠的临床试验,包括尽管证据有限但在某些情况下使用的药物。
科研与实践启示
审查表明,BSA 的开发对于大流行防范至关重要,因为它可以快速应对新出现的病毒威胁。它还强调了固定剂量组合的重要性以及进一步研究不同 RNA 病毒家族中 抗病毒药物 活性的生物决定因素的必要性。

Towards broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals against RNA viruses

Authors: Hendrik Jan Thibaut, Dirk Jochmans, Christophe Van Dijck, Manon Laporte, Laurens Liesenborghs, Johan Neyts
Original abstract (English)
Nature Reviews Microbiology nature reviews microbiology https://doi.org/10.1038/s41579-026-01372-1 Review article Check for updates Towards broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals against RNA viruses Hendrik Jan Thibaut 1, Dirk Jochmans 1,3, Christophe Van Dijck2,3, Manon Laporte 1,3, Laurens Liesenborghs 2 & Johan Neyts1 Abstract Small molecule antiviral drugs have transformed the management of infections such as those caused by HIV, hepatitis B virus, hepatitis C virus, herpesviruses and influenza viruses. In this Review, we discuss the rationale for building a diversified portfolio of broader-spectrum antivirals against RNA viruses with activity spanning multiple genera, entire families or even crossing viral families. e-mail: johan.neyts@kuleuven.be Nature Reviews Microbiology Review article Alongside vaccine development, sustained investment in antiviral drug discovery is therefore essential. In this Review, we discuss the rationale for the development of BSAs and provide a non-exhaustive overview of approved or experi- mental antiviral drugs active against viruses with an RNA genome. Most of these viruses are RNA viruses — the focus of this Review — including many emerging and clinically important pathogens10–12. Effective antiviral therapeutics remain unavailable for many other often life-threatening viral infections, many of which are caused by RNA viruses. The development of potent, safe and orally available antivirals is therefore essential both to address current unmet medical needs and to strengthen epidemic and pandemic preparedness. We provide a non-exhaustive overview of approved and investigational antiviral agents active against RNA viruses and discuss the biological determinants that underlie their antiviral spectrum. Finally, we outline the clinica...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the development of broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals (BSAs) against RNA viruses, with a particular emphasis on measles virus. The central question is whether BSAs can be developed to target multiple RNA virus genera, families, or even cross families, and how this approach can address the challenges of treating viral infections with limited therapeutic options.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of antiviral drug development, discussing the rationale for BSAs, their biological determinants, and clinical development frameworks. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or study counts, but it outlines the general approach to identifying and evaluating antiviral agents against RNA viruses.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the development of BSAs is a promising strategy to address the unmet medical needs for antiviral treatments against RNA viruses, including measles virus. It highlights the importance of targeting conserved viral components to achieve broad antiviral activity and emphasizes the need for clinical frameworks to evaluate these drugs effectively.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that the term 'broad-spectrum' antiviral lacks a clear definition, and it proposes the use of 'broader-spectrum' instead. It also identifies gaps in the evidence, such as the limited preclinical data for many antiviral agents and the lack of robust clinical trials for some drugs, including those that were used in certain settings despite limited evidence.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that the development of BSAs is critical for pandemic preparedness, as it allows for rapid response to emerging viral threats. It also highlights the importance of fixed-dose combinations and the need for further research into the biological determinants of antiviral activity across different RNA virus families.

多发性硬化症和病毒:《危险关系》。

Multiple Sclerosis and Viruses: « Liaisons Dangereuses ».
作者： Samanta Plavina, Daniela Malakovska, Jolanta Kalnina, Ilva Trapina, Elina Leonova, Mohammed Habib, Susanne Schifmann, Jegors Paramonovs, Nikolajs Sjakste, Yegor S Vassetzky
摘要中文翻译摘要 多发性硬化症(MS)是一种复杂的、多因素的神经退行性疾病,其病因仍部分难以捉摸。多种风险因素与多发性硬化症的发生有关,包括遗传倾向和各种环境压力因素;然而,最近,病毒感染作为一个重要的环境触发因素受到了广泛关注。尽管 Epstein-Barr 病毒 (EBV) 和 MS 易感性之间存在明确的联系,但单一病原体模型无法清楚地描述驱动该疾病的分子级联反应,这表明从感染到自身免疫反应的转变涉及不止一种病毒因子。在这篇综述中,我们研究了这样的推测:不同嗜神经病毒之间的相互作用可能在多发性硬化症中发挥集体致病作用,目前文献对此研究不足。我们总结了关于不同病毒因子作用的相互矛盾的证据,包括疱疹病毒科(人类巨细胞病毒、EBV、单纯疱疹病毒HSV-1和HSV-2以及水痘带状疱疹病毒)、副粘病毒科(麻疹病毒和风疹病毒)、流感病毒、SARS-CoV-2和人内源性逆转录病毒(HERV)。通过共享分子途径、免疫调节和潜在感染激活之间的相互作用,这些病毒可能共同影响多发性硬化症的炎症和脱髓鞘过程。本综述通过这些病原体的累积和相互作用效应评估了病毒相互作用在多发性硬化症中的病因学作用。现有的流行病学、免疫学和实验数据集中在这样一个概念上:病毒共同暴露协同作用,影响遗传易感个体的多发性硬化症易感性。表征这些病毒相互作用为识别新型生物标志物和通过有针对性地控制病毒网络制定预防策略提供了新的研究方向。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了病毒相互作用在多发性硬化症 (MS) 病因学中的潜在作用,重点关注 麻疹病毒 作为目标实体。它研究了病毒共同暴露如何协同影响遗传易感个体的多发性硬化症易感性。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对文献的系统分析,结合了流行病学、免疫学和实验数据。它评估了病毒相互作用在 MS 中的集体致病作用,特别关注 麻疹病毒 及其与其他病原体的相互作用。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,包括 麻疹病毒 在内的病毒共同暴露可能会协同作用,从而影响遗传易感个体的多发性硬化症易感性。它强调需要了解这些相互作用背后的分子机制。
争议与缺口
关于不同病毒因子在多发性硬化症中的作用存在相互矛盾的证据。该综述确定了在理解 麻疹病毒 和其他病原体影响疾病的特定分子途径和免疫机制方面的差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,表征病毒相互作用为识别新型生物标志物和通过有针对性地控制病毒网络制定预防策略提供了一个新的研究方向。

Multiple Sclerosis and Viruses: « Liaisons Dangereuses ».

Authors: Samanta Plavina, Daniela Malakovska, Jolanta Kalnina, Ilva Trapina, Elina Leonova, Mohammed Habib, Susanne Schifmann, Jegors Paramonovs, Nikolajs Sjakste, Yegor S Vassetzky
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Multiple sclerosis (MS) is a complex, multifactorial neurodegenerative disease with an aetiology that is still partially elusive. Several risk factors are associated with MS development including genetic predisposition and various environmental stressors; however, recently, viral infections have gained significant attention as a crucial environmental trigger. Despite the well‐established link between Epstein‐Barr virus (EBV) and MS susceptibility, a single‐pathogen model fails to clearly characterise the molecular cascades driving the disease, suggesting that the transition from infection to autoimmune response involves more than one viral agent. In this review we examine the speculation that the interactions among different neurotropic viruses could serve a collective causative role in MS, which is under‐researched in the literature at present. We summarise contradictory evidence on the role of different viral agents, including the Herpesviridae family (Human Cytomegalovirus, EBV, Herpes Simplex Viruses HSV‐1 and HSV‐2, and Varicella Zoster Virus), Paramyxoviridae family (Measles Virus and Rubella Virus), Influenza virus, SARS‐CoV‐2, and Human Endogenous Retroviruses (HERVs). Through interplay within shared molecular pathways, immune modulation, and activation of latent infections, these viruses may collectively influence the inflammatory and demyelinating processes that characterise MS. This review evaluates the etiological role of viral interplay in MS through the cumulative and interactive effects of these pathogens. The available epidemiological, immunological, and experimental data converge on the concept that viral co‐exposures act synergistically to shape MS susceptibility in genetically predisposed individuals. Characterising these viral interactions presents a new research direction for the identification of novel biomarkers and the development of preventative strategies through the targeted control of viral networks.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review explores the potential role of viral interactions in the etiology of multiple sclerosis (MS), with a focus on the measles virus as a target entity. It examines how viral co-exposures may synergistically influence MS susceptibility in genetically predisposed individuals.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of the literature, incorporating epidemiological, immunological, and experimental data. It evaluates the collective causative role of viral interplay in MS, particularly focusing on the measles virus and its interactions with other pathogens.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that viral co-exposures, including the measles virus, may act synergistically to shape MS susceptibility in genetically predisposed individuals. It highlights the need to understand the molecular mechanisms underlying these interactions.
Controversies and gaps
There is contradictory evidence regarding the role of different viral agents in MS. The review identifies gaps in understanding the specific molecular pathways and immune mechanisms by which the measles virus and other pathogens influence the disease.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that characterising viral interactions presents a new research direction for identifying novel biomarkers and developing preventative strategies through targeted control of viral networks.

非编码RNA介导的新兴病毒与宿主 抗病毒药物防御之间的拉锯战:来自动物病毒的教训

Non-coding RNA-mediated tug-of-war between emerging viruses and host antiviral defense: lessons from animal viruses
作者： Na Chen, Baoge Zhang, Ziding Yu, Guangzhi Tong, Jiayu Jin, Qi Jiang, Xiao Chen
摘要中文翻译新兴动物病毒对牲畜饲养和公共卫生构成持续威胁,强调需要更深入地了解病毒与 宿主 相互作用。然而,宿主非编码RNA(ncRNA)在动物病毒感染过程中的调控作用和保守机制仍然支离破碎,而新出现的动物病毒的ncRNA介导的机制尤其缺乏表征,没有系统的整理或理论总结。研究表明,ncRNA,包括 miRNA、lncRNA 和 circRNA,已成为动物病毒与其 宿主 之间相互作用的重要调节因子。本综述综合了来自猪、家禽、反刍动物、猫、犬和水貂 宿主 的 11 种动物病毒的证据,重点是病毒诱导的 ncRNA 表达及其 抗病毒药物 或前病毒功能的变化。在不同的病毒-宿主系统中,确定了三种重复出现的监管范式:宿主 抗病毒药物防御、病毒劫持和病毒拮抗。宿主细胞可以动态重塑其ncRNA表达,以调节宿主限制因子、前病毒宿主因子和其他ncRNA,从而影响先天免疫信号、病毒复制周期、自噬、细胞凋亡和代谢重编程。相反,动物病毒,包括PEDV、PRRSV、PRV、SVA、AIV、ALV-J、NDV、BVDV、FHV-1、CIV和MEV,已经进化出复杂的策略来劫持宿主 ncRNA网络或拮抗ncRNA介导的抗病毒药物免疫,并重塑细胞内微环境以促进病毒复制。这些范式为理解和预测新兴动物病毒中 ncRNA 介导的调控机制提供了统一的理论体系。它们还为剖析 ASFV、SADS-CoV 和未来未知新兴病毒的致病模式,以及挖掘、筛选和识别潜在的 抗病毒药物 药物靶点提供参考。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注非编码 RNA (ncRNA) 在新兴动物病毒和 宿主 抗病毒药物 防御之间相互作用中的作用,特别强调 麻疹病毒 和相关实体。核心问题是 ncRNA 如何介导病毒和 宿主 免疫应答 之间的动态相互作用,特别是在 麻疹病毒 及其相关病毒的背景下。
证据基础与方法
该综述对包括 麻疹病毒 在内的 11 种动物病毒的证据进行了叙述性综合,涵盖了猪、家禽、反刍动物、猫科动物、犬科动物和水貂等各种 麻疹病毒。证据主要来自于检查病毒诱导的 ncRNA 表达变化及其在 抗病毒药物 或原病毒环境中的功能的研究。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或汇集数据的系统方法。
共识与结论
该综述确定了病毒-宿主 相互作用中的三种反复出现的监管范式:宿主 抗病毒药物 防御、病毒劫持和病毒拮抗。这些范式被提出作为一个统一的理论系统,用于理解新兴动物病毒中 ncRNA 介导的调节机制,包括 麻疹病毒。
争议与缺口
该综述指出,宿主 ncRNA 在动物病毒感染过程中的调控作用和保守机制仍然支离破碎,特别是对于新出现的病毒。对于 麻疹病毒 和相关实体中 ncRNA 介导的机制,没有系统的整理或理论总结,凸显了显着的证据差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,所确定的范式可以作为剖析 麻疹病毒、亚急性硬化性全脑炎 和未来未知新出现病毒的致病模式的参考。它还强调了通过研究 ncRNA 相互作用来挖掘、筛选和识别 抗病毒药物 药物靶点的潜力。

Non-coding RNA-mediated tug-of-war between emerging viruses and host antiviral defense: lessons from animal viruses

Authors: Na Chen, Baoge Zhang, Ziding Yu, Guangzhi Tong, Jiayu Jin, Qi Jiang, Xiao Chen
Original abstract (English)
Emerging animal viruses pose persistent threats to livestock breeding and public health, underscoring the need for a deeper understanding of virus-host interactions. Nevertheless, the regulatory roles and conserved mechanisms of host non-coding RNAs (ncRNAs) during animal virus infections remain fragmented, and ncRNA-mediated mechanisms for emerging animal viruses are particularly poorly characterized, with no systematic collation or theoretical summarization available. Studies indicate that ncRNAs, including miRNAs, lncRNAs, and circRNAs, have emerged as important regulators of the interplay between animal viruses and their hosts. This review synthesizes evidence from eleven animal viruses covering swine, poultry, ruminant, feline, canine, and mink hosts, with emphasis on virus-induced changes in ncRNA expression and their antiviral or proviral functions. Across diverse virus-host systems, three recurring regulatory paradigms are identified: host antiviral defense, viral hijacking, and viral antagonism. Host cells can dynamically reshape their ncRNA expression to regulate host restriction factors, proviral host factors, and other ncRNAs, thereby influencing innate immune signaling, viral replication cycles, autophagy, apoptosis, and metabolic reprogramming. Conversely, animal viruses, including PEDV, PRRSV, PRV, SVA, AIV, ALV-J, NDV, BVDV, FHV-1, CIV, and MEV, have evolved sophisticated strategies to hijack host ncRNA networks or antagonize ncRNA-mediated antiviral immunity, and remodel the intracellular microenvironment to facilitate viral replication. These paradigms provide a unified theoretical system for understanding and predicting ncRNA-mediated regulatory mechanisms in emerging animal viruses. They also offer references for dissecting the pathogenic patterns of ASFV, SADS-CoV, and future unknown emerging viruses, as well as, for mining, screening, and identifying potential antiviral drug targets.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the role of non-coding RNAs (ncRNAs) in the interaction between emerging animal viruses and host antiviral defenses, with a specific emphasis on measles virus and related entities. The central question is how ncRNAs mediate the dynamic interplay between viruses and host immune responses, particularly in the context of measles virus and its related viruses.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of evidence from eleven animal viruses, including measles virus, covering various hosts such as swine, poultry, ruminant, feline, canine, and mink. The evidence is primarily derived from studies examining virus-induced changes in ncRNA expression and their functions in antiviral or proviral contexts. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or systematic methods for pooling data.
Consensus and conclusions
The review identifies three recurring regulatory paradigms in virus-host interactions: host antiviral defense, viral hijacking, and viral antagonism. These paradigms are presented as a unified theoretical system for understanding ncRNA-mediated regulatory mechanisms in emerging animal viruses, including measles virus.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that the regulatory roles and conserved mechanisms of host ncRNAs during animal virus infections remain fragmented, particularly for emerging viruses. There is no systematic collation or theoretical summarization available for ncRNA-mediated mechanisms in measles virus and related entities, highlighting a significant evidence gap.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that the identified paradigms can serve as references for dissecting the pathogenic patterns of measles virus, subacute sclerosing panencephalitis, and future unknown emerging viruses. It also highlights the potential for mining, screening, and identifying antiviral drug targets through the study of ncRNA interactions.

泛美卫生组织评估 麻疹 在美洲宗教群众集会之前传播的风险非常高

PAHO assesses risk of measles spread as very high ahead of religious mass gatherings in the Americas
新闻简报泛美卫生组织 (PAHO) 发布了一项区域风险评估,表明在即将举行的宗教群众集会之前,麻疹 在美洲传播的风险非常高。这些聚会预计将增加旅行和人口流动,为病毒传播创造更多机会。评估指出,2026年1月4日至10月3日期间,17个国家和地区报告了55,792例麻疹确诊病例,77例死亡,比2025年同期增加4.8倍。危地马拉、墨西哥、美国、秘鲁和加拿大占这些病例的99.5%。泛美卫生组织强调需要加强 疫苗接种 覆盖范围、监测 和快速反应措施来减轻风险。该组织正在通过技术合作、基于实验室的 监测 和基因组监测来支持各国,以增强准备和应对能力。该警告强调了 疫苗接种 缺口的后果以及通过国际旅行输入病例的可能性,特别是在免疫力低下的社区。
查看新闻五要素
时间
泛美卫生组织于2026年10月9日发布了评估报告,最新数据涵盖2026年1月4日至10月3日期间。
地点与对象
美洲,特别是南美洲,危地马拉、墨西哥、美国、秘鲁和加拿大等国家受影响最严重。评估还提到了由于宗教群众集会而可能在整个地区传播的可能性。
事件
泛美卫生组织发布的区域风险评估表明,麻疹 在宗教群众集会之前在美洲传播的风险非常高,这可能会增加旅行和 传播 的机会。
规模、影响与风险
2026年1月4日至10月3日期间,该区域17个国家和地区报告了55,792例麻疹确诊病例和77例死亡。这意味着病例数比2025年同期增加了4.8倍。评估强调了由于麻疹差距和人口流动增加导致麻疹进一步传播的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
泛美卫生组织敦促各国加强 疫苗接种、监测 和快速应对措施。本组织正在支持基于实验室的 监测、基因组监测和技术合作,以改进 暴发 应对和接触者追踪。
A high priorityPublic-health news

PAHO assesses risk of measles spread as very high ahead of religious mass gatherings in the Americas

News Brief
The Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) has issued a regional risk assessment indicating a very high risk of measles spread in the Americas ahead of upcoming religious mass gatherings. These gatherings are expected to increase travel and population movement, creating more opportunities for the virus to spread. The assessment notes that between 4 January and 3 October 2026, 55,792 confirmed measles cases and 77 deaths were reported in 17 countries and territories, marking a 4.8-fold increase compared to the same period in 2025. Guatemala, Mexico, the United States, Peru, and Canada accounted for 99.5% of these cases. PAHO emphasizes the need for stronger vaccination coverage, surveillance, and rapid response measures to mitigate the risk. The organization is supporting countries with technical cooperation, laboratory-based surveillance, and genomic monitoring to enhance preparedness and response capabilities. The warning underscores the consequences of vaccination gaps and the potential for imported cases through international travel, particularly in communities with low immunity.
Original source text (English)

Search Search Home News PAHO assesses risk of measles spread as very high ahead of religious mass gatherings in the Americas PAHO assesses risk of measles spread as very high ahead of religious mass gatherings in the Americas Copied to clipboard Vaccination gaps and increased population movement could facilitate further transmission, underscoring the need for stronger vaccination and surveillance efforts Washington, D.C., 9 October 2026 (PAHO) — With several religious mass gatherings in South America expected to increase national and international travel across the Americas in the coming months, the Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) is urging countries to strengthen vaccination, surveillance and rapid response measures as the risk of measles spread across the Region remains very high. The warning comes in a new PAHO regional risk assessment released amid ongoing outbreaks, persistent gaps in vaccination coverage and the detection of measles cases in new geographic areas. The assessment updates PAHO’s regional evaluation of 5 August 2026, with a specific focus on risks associated with upcoming religious mass gatherings in South America and their potential implications for the Region of the Americas. “Measles is highly contagious, and we are seeing the consequences of gaps in vaccination coverage across the Americas,” said Dr. Jarbas Barbosa, PAHO Director. “People need to protect their children with the very effective and safe measles, mumps and rubella (MMR) vaccine, and countries have to close immunity gaps, strengthen surveillance and ensure that they can detect and respond rapidly to cases, particularly as increased travel and large gatherings create more opportunities for the virus to spread.” According to the latest PAHO data, 55,792 confirmed measles cases and 77 deaths were reported in 17 countries and territories in the Region between 4 January and 3 October 2026. This represents a 4.8-fold increase in cases compared with the same period in 2025. Measles cases have also increased globally in 2026, increasing the likelihood of imported cases through international travel and onward transmission in communities with low immunity. Guatemala reported the largest number of cases, followed by Mexico, the United States, Peru and Canada. Together, these five countries accounted for 99.5% of confirmed cases in the Region. Cases have also been reported in Argentina, Belize, Bolivia, Bonaire, Brazil, Chile, Colombia, Costa Rica, El Salvador, Honduras, Panama and Uruguay. Why mass gatherings increase the risk Measles is highly contagious and can spread rapidly when introduced into populations with low immunity, particularly in crowded settings. People with measles can transmit the virus before the characteristic rash appears, meaning infected travelers may unknowingly expose others. Large gatherings and the movement of people between countries and communities can therefore facilitate the importation and onward spread of the virus. “Anyone planning to attend a large gathering should check that they are protected against measles,” said Dr. Daniel Salas, Executive Manager of Comprehensive Immunization at PAHO. “Even people travelling within their own country may be exposed when large numbers of people from different regions come together. People aged 6 months and older who do not have evidence of vaccination should receive a dose of measles-rubella vaccine before traveling or attending the gathering, preferably at least two weeks in advance.” Vaccination remains the main protection Regional coverage reached 88% for the first dose of the MMR vaccine and 85% for the second dose in 2025, below the 95% coverage needed to prevent outbreaks. Persistent low coverage has increased the number of people susceptible to measles and created pockets of susceptible populations in parts of the Region. These gaps reflect several factors, including setbacks in immunization accumulated during the COVID-19 pandemic, vaccine hesitancy in some communities, operational challenges in delivering vaccination and barriers to accessing health services, particularly among Indigenous peoples, migrants and displaced populations. An estimated 1.6 million children in the Region of the Americas did not receive any dose of measles vaccine in 2025. PAHO supports countries to strengthen preparedness PAHO is working with countries across the Region to strengthen vaccination, surveillance and outbreak response. In South America, the Organization is supporting preparedness for upcoming mass gatherings expected to involve significant national and international population movement. PAHO is also supporting laboratory-based surveillance and genomic monitoring across the Region, while affected countries are receiving technical cooperation to strengthen vaccination, contact tracing and outbreak response. PAHO urges countries to close vaccination gaps Measles can cause serious illness and complications at any age, with infants and adults at greater risk of severe disease and death. Children under five and populations experiencing malnutrition or limited access to health services have been disproportionately affected during recent outbreaks in the Region. With active outbreaks continuing and major gatherings approaching, PAHO is urging countries to use every opportunity to ensure that people who are not protected against measles have access to vaccination, while strengthening surveillance and maintaining the capacity to detect and respond rapidly to cases. The region's approximate 3,300 square miles and 100 miles of coastline are divided into five supervisory districts that are each represented by an elected official who serves on the Board of Supervisors. The Board sets governing policies for and serves as the legislative arm for the unincorporated areas of the County. The County government provides more than 700 public services, including law enforcement, animal control, flu shots, transportation planning, foster care, and more. While some County services – such as law enforcement, road maintenance and repair, and land-use planning – are only provided to the unincorporated areas, many are an extension of the State and are also provided to people in the incorporated cities. 宾夕法尼亚州的 麻疹 暴发 是该国三十年来最大的 - NBC 新闻

Pennsylvania’s measles outbreak is the country’s largest in three decades - NBC News
新闻简报截至2026年10月5日,宾夕法尼亚州正面临三十年来最大规模的麻疹 暴发,确诊病例1,004例,住院治疗198例。据报道,已有五人死亡,其中包括一名患有严重脑部炎症的青少年和两名婴儿。暴发 于 4 月下旬开始,尽管 疫苗接种 有所增加,但没有显示出放缓的迹象。近三分之一的病例涉及儿童,其中一些人出现了严重的神经损伤和肝脏并发症。暴发 集中在兰开斯特县,该县是全国阿米什人口最多的县,该县历来 疫苗接种 率较低。迄今为止,宾夕法尼亚州卫生部已注射了 8,843 剂 MMR 疫苗,但官员警告说,这不足以遏制疫情。南卡罗来纳州的 暴发 于 2026 年 4 月结束,此前是约 30 年来最大的一次,并通过 疫苗接种 的大幅上涨得到遏制。然而,宾夕法尼亚州目前的局势仍未得到解决,人们担心病毒的传播和对弱势群体的影响。
查看新闻五要素
时间
暴发于2026年4月下旬开始,截至2026年10月5日,没有显示出放缓的迹象。宾夕法尼亚州卫生部于 2026 年 10 月 5 日报告了病例,南卡罗来纳州的 暴发 于 2026 年 4 月结束。
地点与对象
暴发 以宾夕法尼亚州兰开斯特县为中心,该县是全国阿米什人口最多的地区。它影响了广泛的人群,包括儿童和成人,其中近三分之一的病例是儿童。
事件
宾夕法尼亚州正在经历 麻疹 暴发,这已成为美国最大的 麻疹。宾夕法尼亚州卫生部报告称,麻疹 正在持续进行,没有放缓的迹象。
规模、影响与风险
截至 2026 年 10 月 5 日,宾夕法尼亚州已有 1,004 例 麻疹 病例,其中 198 人住院治疗。已有 5 人死亡,其中包括一名青少年、一名患有哮喘的 40 岁妇女和两名婴儿。暴发还导致一些患者出现严重的神经损伤和继发细菌感染。
应对、状态与不确定性
宾夕法尼亚州卫生部迄今已接种 8,843 剂 MMR 疫苗。暴发 正在进行中,卫生官员对某些社区的 疫苗接种 比率较低感到担忧。南卡罗来纳州的 暴发 通过 疫苗接种 的激增得到遏制,但宾夕法尼亚州目前的情况仍未得到解决。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Pennsylvania’s measles outbreak is the country’s largest in three decades - NBC News

News Brief
As of October 5, 2026, Pennsylvania is facing its largest measles outbreak in three decades, with 1,004 confirmed cases and 198 hospitalizations. Five deaths have been reported, including a teenager with severe brain inflammation and two babies. The outbreak began in late April and shows no signs of slowing, despite an increase in vaccinations. Nearly a third of the cases involve children, some of whom have developed severe neurological damage and liver complications. The outbreak is concentrated in Lancaster County, home to the nation’s largest Amish population, which historically has lower vaccination rates. The Pennsylvania Department of Health has administered 8,843 MMR doses so far, but officials warn that this is insufficient to halt the epidemic. The outbreak in South Carolina, which ended in April 2026, was previously the largest in about 30 years and was contained through a significant vaccination surge. However, the current situation in Pennsylvania remains unresolved, with concerns about the spread and impact on vulnerable populations.
Original source text (English)
The measles virus under a microscope. The virus has infected 1,004 people in Pennsylvania. It's now the largest outbreak in decades. BSIP / Universal Images Group via Getty Oct. 5, 2026, 9:37 PM UTC By Erika Edwards Pennsylvania’s measles outbreak is now the largest in the U.S. in three decades. On Monday, the state surpassed South Carolina’s outbreak earlier this year, which topped out at 997. That outbreak was contained within six months. Pennsylvania’s outbreak, which began in late April, shows no sign of slowing. “I would bet that this goes on for at least another three to six months,” said Dr. John Goldman, an infectious disease expert at the University of Pittsburgh Medical Center. “We have seen an increase in vaccinations, but we aren’t seeing enough people get vaccinated to stop the epidemic.” On Monday, the Pennsylvania Department of Health reported 1,004 measles cases, with 198 hospitalizations. Nearly a third of the cases have been in children. Among the pediatric measles cases, 18% have needed hospitalization. Dehydration has become a common problem, particularly among babies and younger children, said Dr. Evan Shirey, a pediatric infectious disease physician at Penn State Health Golisano Children’s Hospital in Hershey. Doctors are also seeing young patients with liver problems. “One of the more common things that the measles virus can do is cause hepatitis or infection of the liver,” Shirey said. “These children will feel very sick, very nauseous, with abdominal pain and vomiting, and they can’t take a bottle or can’t keep food or liquid down.” Other children are developing severe and irreversible neurological damage because of brain inflammation, or encephalitis, Shirey said. “In some of these cases, it’s a child who can’t recognize their own mother, who’s disoriented and confused,” Shirey said. Still others have developed secondary bacterial infections. Five people in the state have died, according to the health department. Those people include a teenager with severe brain inflammation and a 40-year-old woman with a history of asthma, as well as two babies. One of those babies was a newborn who died after his mother went into labor a month early. There are few details about the fifth person who died, other than the person hadn’t been vaccinated and lived in Lancaster County, where the bulk of measles cases are. Lancaster County is home to the nation’s largest Amish population, with approximately 44,000 residents as of 2025 . There is nothing in the religion that is inherently anti-vaccine, but historically, their vaccination rates have been lower than those of the general population, Goldman said. According to an NBC News investigation , 88.5% of kindergartners in 2024 had the measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) vaccine. A vaccination rate of at least 95% is the accepted level of herd immunity needed to protect against a measles outbreak. “There’s nothing special about Lancaster. The exact same thing could happen in any county in the country that has a large percentage of unvaccinated people,” Goldman said. “If it hasn’t happened yet, it’s only because measles hasn’t gotten into that county yet.” The outbreak in South Carolina, which ended in April, was previously the largest in approximately 30 years , according to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. That outbreak was linked to a community of people who emigrated from Ukraine over the past few decades. State health officials credited a massive surge in vaccinations with bringing the outbreak to a halt. In the northwestern corner of the state, where the outbreak was centered, nearly 15,000 MMR vaccinations were given during the South Carolina outbreak, up 82% compared with the previous year, according to the South Carolina Department of Public Health . As of Monday, the Pennsylvania health department said it’s given just 8,843 MMR doses so far in response to the outbreak. Ohio is also dealing with a growing measles outbreak along the border it shares with Pennsylvania. As of Thursday, Ohio had logged 210 measles cases , with 13 hospitalizations. Most cases are concentrated in the northeastern part of the state, in Geauga, Ashtabula and Wayne counties
View five news elements
Time
The outbreak began in late April 2026 and as of October 5, 2026, it has shown no sign of slowing. The Pennsylvania Department of Health reported the cases on October 5, 2026, and the outbreak in South Carolina ended in April 2026.
Location and population
The outbreak is centered in Lancaster County, Pennsylvania, which is home to the nation’s largest Amish population. It has affected a wide population, including children and adults, with nearly a third of the cases in children.
Event
Pennsylvania is experiencing a measles outbreak that has become the largest in the U.S. The outbreak is reported by the Pennsylvania Department of Health and is confirmed as ongoing with no signs of slowing.
Scale, impact and risk
As of October 5, 2026, there have been 1,004 measles cases in Pennsylvania, with 198 hospitalizations. Five people have died, including a teenager, a 40-year-old woman with asthma, and two babies. The outbreak has also led to severe neurological damage and secondary bacterial infections in some patients.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Pennsylvania Department of Health has administered 8,843 MMR doses so far. The outbreak is ongoing, and health officials are concerned about the low vaccination rates in certain communities. The outbreak in South Carolina was contained through a surge in vaccinations, but the current situation in Pennsylvania remains unresolved.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

麻疹 宾夕法尼亚州今年病例数达到 1,078 例,大脑并发症可能会在康复数周或数年后出现 - Medical Daily

Measles Brain Complications Can Surface Weeks or Years After Recovery as Pennsylvania Cases Reach 1,078 This Year - Medical Daily
新闻简报《医学日报》的文章报道称,宾夕法尼亚州已确认 2026 年有 5 例 麻疹 死亡,而 CDC 的全国统计仅列出了 2 例。它强调 麻疹 脑部并发症可能在康复后数周或数年出现,强调了与该疾病相关的长期风险。这篇文章没有提供有关卫生当局采取的调查、测试或应对措施的具体细节。该信息基于截至 2026 年 10 月 9 日的最新数据,重点关注确诊病例及其对公共卫生的影响。该文章提醒人们 麻疹 的潜在严重性以及 疫苗接种 对于预防此类并发症和死亡的重要性。

2026 年,宾夕法尼亚州确认有 5 例 麻疹 死亡,而 CDC 的全国统计则列出了 2 例。文章提到,麻疹 脑部并发症可能会在康复后数周或数年出现。
应对、状态与不确定性
该文章报告了宾夕法尼亚州已确认的 麻疹 死亡人数以及 CDC 的统计数据,但没有提供有关调查、测试或采取的应对措施的详细信息。

Measles Brain Complications Can Surface Weeks or Years After Recovery as Pennsylvania Cases Reach 1,078 This Year - Medical Daily

News Brief
The article from Medical Daily reports that Pennsylvania has confirmed five measles deaths in 2026, while the CDC's national count lists only two. It highlights that measles brain complications can emerge weeks or years after recovery, emphasizing the long-term risks associated with the disease. The piece does not provide specific details about the investigation, testing, or response measures taken by health authorities. The information is based on the latest data available as of October 9, 2026, and the focus is on the confirmed cases and their implications for public health. The article serves as a reminder of the potential severity of measles and the importance of vaccination to prevent such complications and deaths.

切斯特县高中因 麻疹 案件周五停课

Chester County high school is closed Friday due to measles case
新闻简报宾夕法尼亚州切斯特县的牛津地区高中因社区出现确诊的 麻疹 病例,于 2026 年 10 月 9 日星期五关闭。学校正在核实员工的免疫力文件,任何未接种疫苗或无法证明免疫力的人都禁止参加学校或学校活动,并且必须隔离 21 天。宾夕法尼亚州正在经历 35 年来最严重的 麻疹 麻疹,截至周五上午,报告有 1,078 例病例和 207 例住院治疗。切斯特县已报告 86 例病例,卫生官员警告费城国际机场和费城儿童医院新生儿重症监护室存在潜在暴露风险。作为预防措施,学校采取了加强清洁和消毒措施。CDC 仅列出了全国范围内的 2 例 麻疹 死亡病例,但宾夕法尼亚州今年报告了 5 例与 麻疹 相关的死亡病例。卫生官员敦促未接种疫苗的个人接种疫苗,因为两剂 MMR 疫苗 预防该病毒的有效率为 97%。
查看新闻五要素
时间
牛津地区高中因确诊的 麻疹 病例而于周五停课。2026年10月9日宣布学校关闭,学校社区确诊麻疹病例。宾夕法尼亚州的 暴发 仍在进行中,截至周五上午,报告了 1,078 例病例和 207 例住院治疗,切斯特县报告了 86 例病例。
地点与对象
位于宾夕法尼亚州切斯特县的牛津地区高中是受影响的地点。学校社区,包括学生和教职员工,是受影响的人群。
事件
牛津地区高中社区报告了一例确诊的 麻疹 病例,导致学校于周五关闭。学校还在核实员工的豁免文件。卫生官员警告费城国际机场和费城儿童医院新生儿重症监护室存在潜在的暴露风险。
规模、影响与风险
截至周五上午,宾夕法尼亚州已报告 39 个县有 1,078 例 麻疹 病例,其中 207 例住院。切斯特县已报告 86 例病例。牛津地区高中的 麻疹 病例促使人们加强清洁和消毒措施。卫生官员敦促未接种疫苗的个人接种疫苗,因为两剂 MMR 疫苗 预防该病毒的有效率为 97%。
应对、状态与不确定性
切斯特县卫生部门正在联系可能接触过该病毒的人。学校关闭是作为预防措施而实施的。学区有学生的 疫苗接种 记录存档。CDC 仅列出了全国范围内的 2 例 麻疹 死亡病例,但宾夕法尼亚州今年报告了 5 例与 麻疹 相关的死亡病例。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Chester County high school is closed Friday due to measles case

News Brief
Oxford Area High School in Chester County, Pennsylvania, was closed on Friday, October 9, 2026, due to a confirmed measles case in its community. The school is verifying staff immunity documentation, and anyone not vaccinated or unable to prove immunity is prohibited from attending school or school functions and must quarantine for 21 days. Pennsylvania is experiencing its largest measles outbreak in 35 years, with 1,078 cases and 207 hospitalizations reported as of Friday morning. Chester County has reported 86 cases, and health officials have warned of potential exposures at the Philadelphia International Airport and the neonatal intensive care unit of the Children's Hospital of Philadelphia. Enhanced cleaning and disinfection measures were taken at the school as a precaution. The CDC has only listed two measles deaths nationwide, but Pennsylvania has reported five measles-related deaths this year. Health officials have urged unvaccinated individuals to get vaccinated, as two doses of the MMR vaccine are 97% effective at preventing the virus.
Original source text (English)

All rights reserved More Health: Health News Parenting Wellness Healthy Eating Mental Health Addiction Newsletters October 09, 2026 Chester County high school is closed Friday due to measles case Oxford Area High School has been directed to verify its staff’s immunity documentation. Anyone who is not protected from the virus cannot attend school for 21 days. By Molly McVety PhillyVoice Staff Illness Measles from Ken Ruinard/USA Today Network via Reuters Connect Oxford Area High School is closed Friday due to a confirmed case of measles in its school community. Above, a vial of the measles, mumps and rubella vaccine. Read More Oxford Area High School is closed Friday due to a confirmed measles case in the school community. The Chester County Health Department is contacting people who may have been exposed to the virus, David A. Woods, superintendent of Oxford Area School District, said in a letter to families Thursday. The school was closed so officials can verify immunity documentation for all staff members who work in the building. The district has students' vaccination records on file. MORE: Pennsylvania's ban on most e-cigarette products begins soon Anyone who is not vaccinated against measles, or able to prove their vaccination status, will not be allowed to attend school or school functions and must quarantine for 21 days. "We recognize that situations such as this can be unsettling, especially when they involve the health of children," Woods said. "Please know that we are committed to keeping our school community informed, supporting our families and working in partnership with public health officials to help protect the health and safety of everyone in our schools." Enhanced cleaning and disinfection was completed at the school "out of an abundance of caution," Woods said. Measles is a highly contagious virus that spreads through the air when infected people cough, sneeze or talk. People who are not protected from measles can contract it by breathing in air up to two hours after someone with measles has left the area. Measles is contagious between four days before and four days after a rash develops. Health officials have urged people who have never had measles or have not received two doses of the measles, mumps and rubella vaccine to get vaccinated. Two doses of the vaccine are 97% effective at preventing the virus, according to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. Oxford Area High School's measles case comes as the state has been grappling with the United States' largest measles outbreak in 35 years. As of Friday morning, the state's measles dashboard shows 1,078 cases and 207 hospitalizations in 39 counties this year. Chester County has reported 86 cases. Philadelphia has not reported any case this year, but health officials have warned of potential exposures at the Philadelphia International Airport and the neonatal intensive care unit of the Children's Hospital of Philadelphia. Last week, the state reported that an unvaccinated Lancaster County resident tested positive for measles and died, marking the state's fifth measles-associated death this year. Pennsylvania defines measles-related deaths as those that occur within 30 days of the onset of symptoms from a lab-confirmed measles case and are not due to unrelated causes. Pennsylvania's outbreak has provoked a clash between Gov. Josh Shapiro and Health and Human Services Secretary Robert F. Kennedy Jr., who has posted debunked claims that MMR vaccines contain fetal tissue and accused Shapiro of fabricating deaths. The CDC has only listed two measles deaths nationwide. Shapiro has said Pennsylvania will not accept any federal funding to fight the measles outbreak until the CDC acknowledges its other measles-related deaths. Oxford Area High School closed Friday due to a confirmed measles case. The school closure was announced on October 9, 2026, and the measles case was confirmed in the school community. The outbreak in Pennsylvania has been ongoing, with 1,078 cases and 207 hospitalizations reported as of Friday morning, and Chester County has reported 86 cases.
Location and population
Oxford Area High School, located in Chester County, Pennsylvania, is the affected location. The school community, including students and staff, is the affected population.
Event
A confirmed measles case was reported in the Oxford Area High School community, leading to the closure of the school on Friday. The school is also verifying staff immunity documentation. Health officials have warned of potential exposures at the Philadelphia International Airport and the neonatal intensive care unit of the Children's Hospital of Philadelphia.
Scale, impact and risk
As of Friday morning, Pennsylvania has reported 1,078 measles cases and 207 hospitalizations in 39 counties. Chester County has reported 86 cases. The measles case at Oxford Area High School has prompted enhanced cleaning and disinfection measures. Health officials have urged unvaccinated individuals to get vaccinated, as two doses of the MMR vaccine are 97% effective at preventing the virus.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Chester County Health Department is contacting people who may have been exposed to the virus. The school closure was implemented as a precautionary measure. The district has students' vaccination records on file. The CDC has listed only two measles deaths nationwide, but Pennsylvania has reported five measles-associated deaths this year.
